Conclusion: 窗口充分性结论具有条件依赖性，报告时应明确队列构建、目标定义和特征族；机制可能泛化但具体幅度因数据集而异。

Abstract: How many days of early behavior suffice for subscription churn prediction? In the public KKBox dataset, the early indicator of churn is typically an indicator of someone's contract status; however, when looking in the heavily churned manual-renewal segment, having access to early behavior creates a substantial increase in prediction for that specific segment (PR +0.10 at 120 days). A nine-window sufficiency curve shows a diminishing-returns knee in a 45-90 day band. However, stress-testing over three cohort/task designs shows that this curve is singular to the design being tested; for example, in our test with a moving target, the curve inverts and can shift depending on the feature set used. Therefore, any window-sufficiency claim should state its cohort construction, target definition, and feature families. All evidence is from one music-streaming dataset; the mechanism should generalize but the magnitudes may not.

</details>


### [76] [Interpretable vs Learned Encoders for High-Cardinality Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00477)
*Xiao Han,Jingjing Liu,Moxuan Zheng,Zhen Zhang,Chenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在IEEE-CIS欺诈基准数据集上测试了7种分类编码方法，实体嵌入在AUC-ROC指标上表现最佳（0.9612），与CatBoost（0.9602）无统计差异，但显著优于其他编码器；CatBoost在AUC-PR上领先（0.822 vs 0.793），无编码器在两个指标上同时占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较不同分类编码方法在高基数分类特征上的性能表现，并评估实体嵌入、目标编码、层级分组编码等方法在欺诈检测任务中的有效性。

Method: 在IEEE-CIS欺诈数据集（590,540条记录，3.5%正类，8个高基数列）上测试7种分类编码方法，使用分层5折交叉验证重复3次，5种编码器采用相同的冻结LightGBM学习器进行下游训练，CatBoost和TabNet作为跨范式对比。

Result: 实体嵌入AUC-ROC最高（0.9612），与CatBoost（0.9602）无统计差异，显著优于层级分组编码（0.9548）；AUC-PR上CatBoost领先（0.822 vs 0.793）；无编码器在两个指标上同时占优；TabNet未优于树模型且在小样本场景下性能崩溃。

Conclusion: 实体嵌入通过联合多列表示获得优势，但不同编码器在AUC-ROC和AUC-PR上各有优劣，不存在统一的优胜方案。

Abstract: A total of seven categorical encoding methods were tested on the IEEE-CIS fraud benchmark dataset (590,540 records, 3.5% positives, 8 high-cardinality columns). The encoders were evaluated using a stratified 5-fold cross-validation (CV) with three repetitions. Five of the encoders had identical frozen LightGBM learners in the downstream phase, allowing for controlled comparisons of their performance to each other. CatBoost and TabNet were included as comparisons across paradigms using different learners. The entity embeddings produced the highest AUC-ROC (0.9612), with a statistically significant tie with that of CatBoost (0.9602) and statistically superior to tier group encoding (0.9548), whereas target encoding was only 0.0023 worse than tier group encoding and the auditor-friendly tier boundaries were maintained. Off-the-shelf TabNet did not outperform tree-based pipelines and collapsed under data scarcity. On AUC-PR, CatBoost leads (0.822 vs. 0.793); no encoder dominated both metrics. Per-column analysis confirmed the embedding advantage arises from joint multi-column representation.

</details>


### [77] [PAPA: Online Personalized Active Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00486)
*Anindya Sarkar,Nasik Muhammad Nafi,Isaac Lyngaas,Muralikrishnan Gopalakrishnan Meena,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAPA方法，通过实时用户反馈直接优化扩散模型，无需参数化奖励模型，实现个性化偏好对齐；并进一步提出加速版本EPAPA，提升实际部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在个性化推荐等场景中，扩散模型需要根据用户偏好微调，但传统方法依赖大规模偏好数据训练参数化的奖励模型，这在实践中往往不可行。因此需要一种无需参数化奖励模型、能利用实时用户反馈进行偏好对齐的方法。

Method: 提出PAPA（Personalized Active Preference Alignment），通过变分推断框架，直接利用实时用户反馈优化扩散模型，无需学习参数化的奖励模型。并基于理论分析提出改进版EPAPA，减少计算预算并加速微调过程。

Result: 在多种类别条件任务和细粒度对齐任务上，通过大量实验和消融研究验证了PAPA的有效性。EPAPA在更少计算资源下进一步加速了微调过程。

Conclusion: PAPA及其增强版EPAPA为个性化扩散模型的实时偏好对齐提供了高效、实用的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion models are highly effective at modeling complex data distributions, including images and text. However, in applications like personalized recommender systems, the objective often shifts to modeling specific regions of the distribution that maximize user preferences-initially unknown but gradually uncovered through interactive feedback. This can naturally be framed as a reinforcement learning problem, where the goal is to fine-tune a diffusion model to maximize a reward function based on preferences. However, the main challenge lies in learning a parameterized reward model, which typically requires large-scale preference data-something that is often not feasible in practice. In this work, we introduce Personalized Active Preference Alignment PAPA, a novel method that bypasses the requirement for a parametrized reward model by directly optimizing the diffusion model using real-time user feedback. PAPA enables feedback-efficient preference alignment, drawing inspiration from the variational inference framework. We demonstrate PAPA's effectiveness through extensive experiments and ablation studies across diverse class-conditioned and fine-grained alignment tasks. Additionally, based on theoretical insights, we propose an enhanced fine-tuning strategy, referred to as EPAPA, that requires less computational budget and accelerates the fine-tuning process, further boosting PAPA's suitability for real-world deployment. Our code is made publicly available at https://github.com/NasikNafi/papa.

</details>


### [78] [Flow-Map GRPO: Reinforcement Learning for Few-Step Flow-Map Generators via Anchored Stochastic Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00535)
*Zhiqi Li,Wen Zhang,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow-Map GRPO框架，通过锚定随机流映射组合（ASFMC）机制，为确定性少步流映射生成器引入随机性，使其能够有效进行强化学习后训练对齐，无需修改原始模型参数化。实验证明该方法在FLUX-based文本到图像生成器上显著提升了奖励、感知和任务级指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性少步流映射生成器（如一致性模型、MeanFlow）难以应用强化学习后训练方法，因为这些方法需要随机轨迹和良好的似然比，而现有的SDE随机化技术专为基于速度的采样器设计，不适用于长程流映射。

Method: 提出Flow-Map GRPO框架，核心组件是锚定随机流映射组合（ASFMC），通过基于锚点的条件重采样在保持原始确定性流映射边际概率路径的前提下引入随机性，并推导了单时间和双时间流映射参数化的GRPO目标函数。

Result: 在MeanFlow和sCM等FLUX-based少步文本到图像生成器上的实验表明，Flow-Map GRPO在奖励指标、感知指标和任务级评估指标上均显著提升了预训练确定性流映射模型的性能。

Conclusion: 确定性少步流映射生成器可以在不修改原始模型参数化或不重新训练为原生随机模型的情况下，通过RL后训练方法有效地进行对齐优化。

Abstract: Few-step flow-map generators, such as consistency models and MeanFlow, accelerate sampling by directly learning long-range transport maps between noise and data. However, these models are typically deterministic, which makes them difficult to optimize with reinforcement learning (RL) post-training methods that require stochastic trajectories and well-defined likelihood ratios. Existing SDE-based stochasticization techniques are designed for velocity-based samplers with infinitesimal or finely discretized transitions, and therefore do not directly apply to long-range flow maps. In this work, we propose Flow-Map GRPO, an online RL post-training framework for deterministic few-step flow-map generators. The key component is Anchored Stochastic Flow Map Composition (ASFMC), a path-preserving stochasticization mechanism that introduces randomness through anchor-based conditional resampling while preserving the original marginal probability path of the deterministic flow map. We derive GRPO objectives for both single-time and two-time flow-map parameterizations. Experiments on few-step FLUX-based text-to-image generators, including MeanFlow and sCM, show that Flow-Map GRPO improves pretrained deterministic flow-map models across reward-based, perceptual, and task-level evaluation metrics. Our results demonstrate that deterministic few-step flow-map generators can be effectively aligned with RL post-training without modifying their original model parameterization or retraining them as native stochastic models.

</details>


### [79] [Group-Equivariant Poincaré Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00556)
*Aiden Durrant,Rahul Baburajan,Georgios Leontidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Equivariant Poincaré ResNet，将双曲几何与离散对称群（C₄和D₄）结合，通过几何安全的张量重塑、左正则置换双曲群卷积和联合方向Poincaré中点批归一化，解决双曲网络优化计算量大、边界约束严格以及空间变换被视为不同层级概念的问题，在加速收敛的同时保持边界约束和群等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Poincaré ResNet虽展示了双曲空间视觉表征学习的潜力，但其优化受限于黎曼梯度的计算复杂性和流形的严格边界；同时标准双曲网络将同一物体的空间变换视为不同层级概念，导致参数冗余和信号消失。

Method: 1) 将双曲几何与离散对称群（C₄和D₄）结合；2) 提出几何安全的张量重塑；3) 提出左正则置换用于双曲群卷积；4) 提出联合方向Poincaré中点批归一化。

Result: 嵌入等变性大幅缩减优化空间，加速收敛，同时遵守Poincaré球的边界约束并保持空间-群等变性。

Conclusion: 将群等变性引入双曲空间是可行且有效的，能解决双曲网络优化和参数冗余问题，为双曲几何与对称性结合提供了新范式。

Abstract: While recent advancements like the Poincaré ResNet have demonstrated the potential of learning visual representations directly in hyperbolic space, their optimisation remains hampered by the computationally intensive nature of Riemannian gradients and the strict boundaries of the manifold. Furthermore, standard hyperbolic networks treat spatial transformations of the same object as distinct hierarchical concepts, leading to redundant parameter usage and vanishing signals. We propose Equivariant Poincaré ResNets, combining hyperbolic geometry with discrete symmetry groups ($C_4$ and $D_4$). We identify critical roadblocks in applying Euclidean equivariance to hyperbolic space and propose geometrically safe tensor reshaping, left-regular permutations for hyperbolic group convolutions, and joint-orientation Poincaré Midpoint Batch normalisation. Empirically, embedding equivariance drastically reduces the optimisation space, accelerating convergence while accelerating convergence while respecting the boundary constraints of the Poincaré ball and preserving spatial-group equivariance.

</details>


### [80] [Decision-focused Sparse Tangent Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00581)
*Haeun Jeon,Seunghoon Choi,Hyunglip Bae,Yongjae Lee,Woo Chang Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端决策聚焦学习框架，将夏普比率最大化重构为DPP兼容的凸规划层，并用平滑top-k算子替代离散选择实现精确基数约束，使预测、资产选择与再优化之间能够梯度流动，在四大股票市场中取得了优于传统方法的样本外夏普比率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏切线投资组合优化的基数约束形式是NP-hard问题，且标准predict-then-optimize流程中预测精度与下游投资组合质量常常不一致。

Method: 将夏普比率最大化重构为DPP合规的凸规划层，利用平滑top-k算子实现精确基数约束，使梯度能够流经预测、资产选择和再优化过程。

Result: 在四大主要股票市场中，该方法相比历史基线方法和预测聚焦基线方法取得了更有竞争力甚至更优的样本外夏普比率，在较大资产池中优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 端到端决策聚焦学习方法能有效改善稀疏切线投资组合的优化效果，代码已公开。

Abstract: Sparse tangent portfolio optimization aims to learn an interpretable, low-cardinality portfolio in the tangency direction of the mean-variance frontier. However, the associated cardinality-constrained formulation is NP-hard, and standard predict-then-optimize pipelines often misalign forecasting accuracy with downstream portfolio quality. We propose an end-to-end decision-focused learning framework that reformulates Sharpe ratio maximization as a Disciplined Parametrized Programming (DPP)-compliant convex programming layer and replaces discrete selection with a smooth top-$k$ operator enforcing an exact cardinality $k$. This enables gradient flow through prediction, asset selection, and re-optimization, allowing the predictive model to directly optimize portfolio performance. Across four major equity markets, our method achieves competitive and often superior out-of-sample Sharpe ratios compared with historical and prediction-focused baselines, with particularly strong gains in larger asset universes. Our \href{https://github.com/feuerwerksh/Diffble-card-SR}{code} is publicly available.

</details>


### [81] [Measuring Dead Directions: Decomposing and Classifying Singular Structure off Canonical Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00603)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需梯度下降和对齐的奇异结构测量方法，通过方向Fisher率从冻结的训练网络检查点中直接恢复每个死方向（dead direction）的阶数k，精确得到学习系数1/(2k)，并区分真实奇点与平直规范对称性。该方法适用于Transformer、卷积和归一化层，在多种网络结构中验证了其有效性，并揭示了奇异结构与Watanabe三元组的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在测量训练网络的奇异结构时依赖于规范对齐和梯度下降过程，这限制了其通用性和可操作性。本文旨在设计一种直接、确定性的测量方法，能够在单一冻结检查点上恢复奇异结构的核心不变量，而无需依赖优化器的历史轨迹或参数对齐。

Method: 方法核心是利用方向Fisher率（directional-Fisher rate）从单个冻结检查点恢复每个死方向的阶数k，进而精确得到每方向的学习系数1/(2k)。方法包含一个可插拔的检测器，用于提供Transformer、卷积和归一化层中的死方向。通过类型化的交集将各方向阶数组装为全局系数(λ, m)，与封闭形式匹配。该方法去除了规范对齐和梯度下降的前提条件。

Result: 在构造单元和训练网络（包括微调后的ViT、从头训练的ViT等）上，该方法成功恢复了架构预言的阶数。其中，微调后的ViT的死结构表现为LayerNorm核规范，从头训练的ViT的压缩MLP在其激活阶数处形成节点死亡。对于可枚举的奇异结构，各方向阶数通过类型化交集组装为全局系数(λ, m)，与封闭形式一致。

Conclusion: 该方法在Watanabe三元组框架内映射了其适用范围：阶数决定了通用奇异涨落ν(k)，但训练网络实现的ν低于该值，因为活跃结构吸收了死方向的数据涨落；在单一轨迹假设下，多重性可从主导结构中恢复。该方法将阶数恢复变为确定性的、架构通用的读取过程。

Abstract: We give a descent-free, alignment-free measurement of singular structure on trained networks. At a single frozen checkpoint the read recovers the order $k$ of each dead direction from the directional-Fisher rate, the master invariant from which the per-direction learning coefficient $1/(2k)$ follows exactly, in whatever basis the optimizer left. The same read classifies each direction, separating a genuine singularity, whose order the architecture fixes, from a flat gauge symmetry; the directional-Fisher magnitude settles the cases the order cannot. A pluggable detector supplies the directions for transformer, convolutional, and normalisation layers. The read recovers the architecture-predicted order across constructed cells and trained networks, including a fine-tuned vision transformer whose dead structure is the LayerNorm-kernel gauge and a from-scratch one whose compressed MLP forms a node-death at its activation order. Where the singular structure enumerates, the per-direction orders assemble, through the typed intersection of the loci, into the global coefficient $(λ, m)$ matching the closed form. The method removes the canonical-alignment and descent preconditions of the underlying rate result, turning order-recovery into a deterministic, architecture-general reading. We then map its reach into the Watanabe triple: the order determines the universal singular fluctuation $ν(k)$, though a trained network's realized $ν$ falls below it as the live structure absorbs the dead direction's data fluctuation, and the multiplicity recovers from the dominant structure under a single-locus assumption.

</details>


### [82] [Loss Smoothing for Stable Adaptation Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00634)
*Darshan Patil,Ekaterina Lobacheva,Razvan Pascanu,Sarath Chandar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出损失平滑方法，通过在适应初期插值源目标和目标训练目标，实现更平滑的目标过渡，从而保留有用特征并提升模型在不同分布迁移场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的适应方法直接优化目标目标，导致源训练目标的突然转变，这会扭曲学习到的表示，包括对新任务仍有用的特征。

Method: 损失平滑：在适应开始时对源目标和目标训练目标进行插值，实现平滑过渡。

Result: 在受控监督迁移、预训练视觉适应、离线到在线和在线强化学习、语言模型微调等多个场景中，损失平滑一致地提升了性能。

Conclusion: 更平滑的目标过渡是模型适应中一个广泛适用的工具。

Abstract: In settings such as fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, neural networks are often adapted under distribution shift. Standard adaptation methods typically optimize the target objective directly, inducing an abrupt change from the source training objective. This abrupt transition can distort learned representations, including features that may still be useful for the new task. We investigate whether a more gradual transition can improve adaptation. We propose loss smoothing, a simple approach that interpolates between the source and target training objectives at the start of adaptation. This smooth transition helps to preserve useful features from the source distribution while still enabling the model to specialize to the target distribution. Across controlled supervised shifts, pretrained vision adaptation, offline-to-online and online reinforcement learning, and language model fine-tuning, we find that loss smoothing consistently improves performance, suggesting that smoother objective transitions are a broadly useful tool for model adaptation.

</details>


### [83] [Multi-Label Node Classification with Label Influence Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00671)
*Yifei Sun,Zemin Liu,Bryan Hooi,Yang Yang,Rizal Fathony,Jia Chen,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出标签影响传播（LIP）模型，通过将GNN消息传递分解为传播和变换两个操作，分析并量化标签间的相互影响，构建标签影响图传播高阶影响，动态调整学习过程，在多个基准数据集上超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的多标签节点分类方法未能有效处理非欧几里得图数据中标签之间的复杂相互影响关系。

Method: 将GNN消息传递分解为传播和变换两个操作，分别分析和量化标签间的相关性；基于整合的标签相关性构建标签影响图；通过该图传播高阶影响，动态增强贡献正向的标签、抑制贡献负向的标签。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，所提模型在各种设置下均一致优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: LIP模型有效建模了图数据中标签间的复杂影响关系，显著提升了多标签节点分类的性能。

Abstract: Graphs are a complex and versatile data structure used across various domains, with possibly multi-label nodes playing a particularly crucial role. Examples include proteins in PPI networks with multiple functions and users in social or e-commerce networks exhibiting diverse interests. Tackling multi-label node classification (MLNC) on graphs has led to the development of various approaches. Some methods leverage graph neural networks (GNNs) to exploit label co-occurrence correlations, while others incorporate label embeddings to capture label proximity. However, these approaches fail to account for the intricate influences between labels in non-Euclidean graph data. To address this issue, we decompose the message passing process in GNNs into two operations: propagation and transformation. We then conduct a comprehensive analysis and quantification of the influence correlations between labels in each operation. Building on these insights, we propose a novel model, Label Influence Propagation (LIP). Specifically, we construct a label influence graph based on the integrated label correlations. Then, we propagate high-order influences through this graph, dynamically adjusting the learning process by amplifying labels with positive contributions and mitigating those with negative influence. Finally, our framework is evaluated on comprehensive benchmark datasets, consistently outperforming SOTA methods across various settings, demonstrating its effectiveness on MLNC tasks.

</details>


### [84] [Distributed Online Bandit Submodular Maximization with Bounded Sampling Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00680)
*Bin Du,Chang Liu,Dingqi Zhu,Lintao Ye,Dengfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了划分拟阵约束下的分布式在线子模最大化问题，提出了一种统一算法框架，适用于全信息和赌博机两种反馈模型，实现了次线性(1-1/e)-regret保证，并通过有界随机pipage舍入方案解决了采样违规问题，使得累积采样违规在T下次线性且不可改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线子模最大化研究多集中于集中式设置，而分布式场景下多智能体协作决策的理论与算法尚不充分，尤其是面临划分拟阵约束时如何平衡探索与利用、处理连续松弛与舍入导致的采样违规问题。

Method: 提出统一算法框架，支持全信息和赌博机两种反馈模型；设计有界随机pipage舍入方案来缓解连续松弛和舍入带来的采样违规问题。

Result: 两种反馈模型下均实现次线性(1-1/e)-regret保证；采样违规概率渐近趋于零，累积采样违规在T下次线性，且在特定条件下不可改进。

Conclusion: 所提分布式算法的性能与现有集中式算法相当，数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: We study distributed online submodular maximization under partition matroid constraints, in which multiple agents select a limited number of actions from their own subsets sequentially to maximize the cumulative value of a sequence of objective functions. We develop a unified algorithmic framework that accommodates full-information and bandit feedback models. For both feedback models, we prove that the proposed algorithms achieve sublinear $(1-1/e)$-regret guarantees, which are comparable to those achieved by existing centralized counterparts. Furthermore, to tackle the sampling violation issue caused by continuous relaxation and rounding, we develop a bounded stochastic pipage rounding scheme and show that the probability of sampling violation vanishes asymptotically. As a result, the cumulative sampling violation remains sublinear in $T$, which is further shown to be not improvable under certain conditions. Numerical results validate the theoretical findings in this paper.

</details>


### [85] [AdaBoosting Text Prompts for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00684)
*Seokhee Jin,Changhwan Sung,Sunung Mun,Hoyoung Kim,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Text Prompt Boosting (TPB)，一种基于AdaBoost的框架，通过顺序聚合弱文本提示分类器，显式聚焦难分类和误分类样本，提升预训练视觉语言模型的小样本分类性能，并实现跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小样本文本提示方法在构建提示时未显式关注误分类样本，导致即使增加样本数量，性能提升仍然有限。为了充分利用小样本监督信息，需要一种能针对性处理难分类和误分类样本的提示学习方法。

Method: 提出Text Prompt Boosting (TPB)，借鉴AdaBoost思想，将每个基于文本提示的分类器视为弱学习器，通过顺序迭代聚合这些弱学习器形成强集成模型，在每次迭代中显式关注前一步误分类的困难样本，从而逐步提升整体性能。

Result: 在11个分类基准上，TPB在源模型上提升了准确率，并在迁移到更大、更强的VLM时保持了基于样本量驱动的性能增益，而现有方法难以维持这种提升。

Conclusion: TPB能够保留文本空间中任务固有的、与模型无关的线索，实现鲁棒的跨模型迁移，有效利用小样本监督信息，弥补了现有方法在处理误分类样本方面的不足。

Abstract: The classification accuracy of pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) relies on the quality of the text prompts. Handcrafted templates and Large Language Model (LLM)-generated descriptions not only make predictions more interpretable, but also enable reuse of the same prompts across heterogeneous VLMs. Recent works construct task-adapted text prompts with a small number of labeled images. However, existing few-shot text prompting methods do not explicitly focus on misclassified examples during prompt construction, leading to only marginal improvements even as more shots become available. To fully exploit few-shot supervision, we propose Text Prompt Boosting (TPB), an AdaBoost-inspired framework that treats each text-prompt-based classifier as a weak learner and sequentially aggregates them into a strong ensemble by explicitly targeting hard, misclassified examples. Extensive experiments show that TPB preserves task-intrinsic, model-agnostic cues in text space, enabling robust cross-model transfer. Across eleven classification benchmarks, TPB improves accuracy on the source model and preserves shot-driven gains when transferred to larger, more capable VLMs, where existing methods struggle to sustain such improvements.

</details>


### [86] [Generative Refinement for Low-Budget Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00691)
*Edouard R. Dufour,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPARROW是一种将生成先验与奖励信号完全解耦的黑箱优化算法，使用固定结构化的提案算子，通过基于排名的档案引导，能够在极低评估预算下处理复杂几何形状和不可靠奖励信号，并提供渐近收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑箱优化方法在评估函数存在噪声或容易失败时面临挑战，尤其是当高性能解局限于搜索空间中狭窄、弯曲或不连通的区域时。现有基于生成模型的方法需要大量评估来对齐采样器，在低预算场景下不实用。

Method: SPARROW将生成先验与奖励信号完全解耦，使用任何已知损坏过程并在未评估数据上训练过的采样器作为固定的结构化提案算子，通过基于排名的引导在已评估候选解的档案上进行优化。

Result: 在理论上提供了采样器支持上的渐近收敛保证，在实验上展示了在不可靠奖励和几何复杂景观问题上的强经验性能。

Conclusion: SPARROW能够在极低评估预算下有效导航复杂几何形状、处理不可靠奖励信号，并且可以基于任何固定采样器实现优化。

Abstract: Black-box optimization is a fundamental science and engineering tool that makes it possible to optimize objectives without gradient information. Unfortunately, as it often requires many function evaluations, it can be challenging when each one is costly. This is especially true when the evaluation function is noisy or failure-prone, and when high-performing solutions are confined to thin, curved, or disconnected regions of the search space. Existing methods leveraging generative models to navigate these subspaces are built to sample from reward-aligned distributions. As a result, they require a large number of evaluations to align their sampler effectively, making them impractical in low-budget settings. We propose SPARROW, an algorithm that completely decouples the generative prior from the reward signal. SPARROW can use any sampler with a known corruption process and trained on unevaluated data, as a fixed, structured proposal operator. Optimization proceeds by rank-based guidance over an archive of evaluated candidates. SPARROW can navigate complex geometries, handle unreliable reward signals, and perform effective optimization under very low evaluation budgets. We provide asymptotic convergence guarantees over the sampler support and demonstrate strong empirical performance on problems with unreliable rewards and geometrically complex landscapes.

</details>


### [87] [Detecting the Undetectable: Enhancing Unsupervised time series Anomaly Detection via Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00720)
*Seung Hun Han,Hyeongwon Kang,Jinwoo Park,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于主动学习的框架，通过掩码时间序列重建反馈与极小极大学习策略，显著提升现有无监督异常检测模型在复杂工业时间序列中识别细微噪声异常的性能，在28个测试案例上平均AUC提升12.39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业时间序列数据标注成本高，现有无监督方法难以区分近正常异常与正常模式，且易受噪声污染，亟需一种无需额外标注即可增强无监督模型异常检测能力的方法。

Method: (1) 掩码时间序列重建反馈策略：强制模型学习鲁棒的时序依赖关系；(2) 极小极大学习策略：对正常与异常样本差异化处理，提升模型对细微噪声模式的捕捉能力；通过主动学习迭代优化无监督模型。

Result: 在4个多变量时间序列数据集、7种无监督骨干模型、28个测试案例上，所提框架相比原始模型AUC平均提升12.39%，且可即插即用地集成到现有基于重建的无监督异常检测系统中。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了无监督异常检测在细微噪声异常上的局限性，具有良好的通用性和集成性，为工业时间序列异常检测提供了一种低成本、高性能的增强方案。

Abstract: Despite the increasing sophistication of industrial AI systems, the ability to reliably detect subtle and noisy anomalies in complex time series data remains a critical yet unresolved challenge. In large-scale industrial applications, labeling time series data is often prohibitively expensive and time-consuming, making unsupervised learning a practical and widely adopted approach. However, existing unsupervised methods frequently struggle to distinguish near-normal anomalies from normal patterns and are vulnerable to noise contamination within normal samples. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework that leverages active learning to iteratively enhance the performance of unsupervised models. Our framework's core contributions are (1) a masked time-series reconstruction feedback strategy that forces the model to learn robust temporal dependencies, and (2) a minimax learning strategy that promotes robustness by differentially treating normal and abnormal samples. This process encourages the model to better capture the dynamics of subtle and noisy patterns. The proposed framework is evaluated across 28 test cases involving four multivariate time-series datasets and seven unsupervised backbone models. Experimental results demonstrate a 12.39% improvement in AUC compared to the original models, confirming that our method can be readily integrated into existing unsupervised reconstruction-based anomaly detection systems to significantly enhance their performance.

</details>


### [88] [LLM-Guided ODE Discovery and Parameter Inference from Small-Cohort Aggregate Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00733)
*Hanning Yang,Meropi Karakioulaki,Lennart Purucker,Tim Litwin,Cristina Has,Moritz Hess*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AgentODE是一个端到端框架，利用LLM提出候选ODE结构，并通过工具增强的推理代理仅基于群体级摘要统计量迭代优化参数分布，从而在数据稀疏、噪声大且受隐私约束的罕见疾病场景中实现机制建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在罕见疾病中，ODE模型结构和参数通常未知，个体级数据稀缺、噪声大、异质性强且受隐私约束，现有方法无法仅从群体级摘要统计量中联合发现ODE结构并细化参数分布。

Method: AgentODE框架包含两个核心组件：1）LLM提出候选ODE结构；2）工具增强的推理代理通过诊断-更新循环，仅基于群体级摘要统计量迭代优化参数分布。

Result: 在三个跨领域基准问题和两个临床数据集（包括仅46名患者231个观测值的罕见病RDEB）上，AgentODE在所有设置中均恢复了功能一致的ODE结构。在RDEB实验中，从摘要统计量推理促进了符合机理的结构发现，而访问个体级数据的基线方法尽管预测性能更好，却恢复了不合理结构。

Conclusion: AgentODE开启了直接基于群体级摘要统计量对罕见疾病进行机制建模的新可能性，克服了传统上数据稀缺和隐私约束对此类分析的限制。

Abstract: Mechanistic modeling via ordinary differential equations (ODEs) provides interpretable descriptions of complex dynamics and enables inference of underlying mechanisms, which is particularly valuable in clinical settings. However, in rare diseases, both the structure and parameters of the model are typically unknown, while individual-level data is scarce, noisy, heterogeneous, and subject to privacy constraints. In such settings, population-level summary statistics provide a practical privacy-preserving data representation, while capturing heterogeneity further requires modeling parameters as distributions rather than fixed values. Yet no existing method jointly discovers ODE structure and refines parameter distributions solely from summary statistics. We present AgentODE, an end-to-end framework that addresses this gap. An LLM proposes candidate ODE structures, while a tool-augmented inference agent iteratively refines parameter distributions through a diagnosis--update loop, operating on population-level summary statistics alone. We evaluate AgentODE on three benchmark problems across different fields and two clinical datasets, including the rare disease recessive dystrophic epidermolysis bullosa (RDEB), with only 231 observations across 46 patients. AgentODE recovers functionally consistent ODE structures across all settings, and experiments on RDEB demonstrates that in sparse and noisy data settings reasoning from summary statistics promotes mechanistically principled structure discovery, whereas baselines with individual-level data access recover implausible structures despite better predictive performance. AgentODE opens new possibilities for mechanistic modeling of rare diseases directly from population-level summary statistics, where data scarcity and privacy constraints have traditionally limited such analyses.

</details>


### [89] [MosaicKV: Serving Long-Context LLM with Dynamic Two-D KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00760)
*Sheng Qiang,Ruiwei Chen,Yinpeng Wu,Jinyu Gu,Zhichao Hua,Yubin Xia,Binyu Zang,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MosaicKV是一种针对超长上下文LLM服务的动态二维KV缓存压缩系统，通过在序列和通道两个维度上进行细粒度压缩，结合高效的缓存管理机制，在保持低精度损失的同时显著提升服务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩技术通常只针对序列维度或通道维度单一维度进行压缩，随着上下文窗口扩展，单一维度压缩的收益有限；直接应用二维压缩会导致显著的精度损失。

Method: MosaicKV采用动态二维压缩策略，利用KV缓存内元素重要性分布的非均匀性，以KV缓存段为粒度识别重要元素并选择压缩策略；同时引入压缩KV缓存管理机制，利用未充分使用的GPU和CPU资源来维护压缩后的KV缓存并加速注意力计算。

Result: 在H800 GPU上，MosaicKV相比未压缩基线实现了高达16倍注意力加速、4.8倍更低的解码延迟和7.3倍更高的吞吐量，同时内存使用减少3倍，在LongBench和RULER上平均精度损失仅1.76%。

Conclusion: MosaicKV通过动态二维压缩和高效的缓存管理，有效解决了长上下文LLM服务中KV缓存内存瓶颈问题，在保持高精度的同时实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Long-context LLM services now sustain prompts with hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens, making the key-value (KV) cache a first-order serving cost. Because the cache grows linearly with context length, it can exhaust GPU memory, force smaller batches, and reduce serving throughput. Prior KV cache compression techniques typically target only the sequence dimension or only the channel dimension, which leaves limited headroom as context windows scale. Compressing both dimensions promises higher memory reduction, but applying the two forms of compression directly leads to significant accuracy loss.
  This paper introduces MosaicKV, a dynamic two-D (dimensional) KV cache compression system for extremely long-context serving. MosaicKV uses dynamic two-D compression to address the accuracy challenge, exploiting the non-uniform importance distribution of elements within the KV cache. Instead of applying one compression pattern globally, MosaicKV identifies important elements for each KV vector and selects compression strategies at the granularity of KV cache segments. To address the performance challenge, where fine-grained sparsity and compression management overhead can offset the gains from compression, MosaicKV introduces compressed KV cache management. This mechanism uses underutilized GPU and CPU resources to maintain compressed KV caches and accelerate attention computation.
  Evaluation on an H800 GPU with multiple LLMs shows that MosaicKV delivers up to 16x attention speedup, 4.8x lower decode latency, and 7.3x higher throughput than the uncompressed baseline. At the same time, it reduces memory usage by 3x and incurs only 1.76% average accuracy loss on LongBench and RULER.

</details>


### [90] [Accelerating Discrete Diffusion Models with Parallel-In-Time Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00773)
*Yu Yao,Huanjian Zhou,Andi Han,Wei Huang,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Picard迭代方法并行化吸收型离散扩散模型的τ-leaping采样算法，在连续时间马尔可夫链框架下实现了时间维度上的并行加速，时间复杂度从O(d log S)降低到O(log(d log S)·log d)，实验表明在合成数据和真实数据（图像/文本）上均获得显著的运行时加速（最高7-9倍），且采样质量保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型的采样过程本质上是顺序的，加速采样对于提升推理效率至关重要。

Method: 在连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）框架下，利用τ-leaping算法的连续时间随机积分形式和Picard迭代方法，实现时间维度上的并行采样加速。

Result: 吸收设置下τ-leaping算法的时间复杂度从O(d log S)降至O(log(d log S)·log d)；合成数据上获得7-9倍运行时加速；图像/文本任务中以50%更少的NFE和1.45-1.86倍运行时加速保持相同采样质量。

Conclusion: 本研究扩展了离散扩散模型在高效并行推理方面的潜力，对分子结构生成和语言生成等应用具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are widely used for learning and generating discrete distributions. As the generation process is inherently sequential, the acceleration of sampling is of significant importance. In this work, we parallelize the mainstream $τ$-leaping algorithm for absorbing discrete diffusion in a Continuous-Time Markov Chain (CTMC) framework. By leveraging the continuous-time stochastic integral form of the $τ$-leaping algorithm and the Picard iteration method, we achieve parallel-in-time sampling acceleration and provide a proof of exponential-factorial convergence for our algorithm. We improve the overall time complexity of $τ$-leaping under absorbing settings from ${\mathcal{O}}(d \log S)$ to ${\mathcal{O}}(\log (d\log S)\cdot \log d)$ with respect to NFE. Empirically, our method shows consistent acceleration across synthetic and real-data settings. The new sampler achieves at most $7$--$9\times$ runtime speedup for synthetic distribution, and maintains the same quality with $50\%$ fewer NFE and $1.45$--$1.86\times$ runtime speedups in image/text tasks on a single GPU. Our research expands the potential of discrete diffusion models for efficient parallel inference, with broader implications for applications such as molecular structure and language generation.

</details>


### [91] [Which Metric Reflects the Spelling Rate Accuracy in Event-Related Potential-Based Brain-Computer Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00794)
*Okba Bekhelifi,Naoual El Djouher Mebtouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了ERP-BCI系统中，拼写率与13种评估指标的相关性，发现Brier分数、MCC、ROC AUC、PR AUC、AP和pAUC等指标最能反映用户拼写表现，推荐在这些系统中使用这些指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BCI系统常用损失和准确率作为评估指标，但对ERP范式而言，拼写率直接影响信息传输速率(ITR)的估计，且ERP-BCI数据存在类别不平衡问题，需要能处理不平衡数据的指标。

Method: 通过两个数据集（LARESI ERP数据集和OpenBMI ERP数据集），研究拼写率与13种评估指标的相关性，分析不同指标反映用户拼写表现的能力及其受试验重复次数的影响。

Result: Brier分数、MCC、ROC AUC、PR AUC、AP和pAUC等指标与拼写率相关性最强，最能反映用户拼写表现。

Conclusion: 建议研究者和实践者在ERP-BCI实验中报告Brier分数、MCC以及能处理类别不平衡的ROC AUC、PR AUC、AP和pAUC等指标。

Abstract: For predictive models, the often-reported performance metrics are the loss and accuracy. In synchronous Brain- Computer Interface (BCI) systems, these metrics are informative for most BCI paradigms; however, for Event-Related Potential (ERP) applications the spelling rate, which measures the number of characters correctly selected is more important as it influences the estimation of information transfer rate (ITR) and any related metric measuring spelling performance. Moreover, ERP-based BCIs hold imbalanced data class distributions, which require reporting metrics that can handle the imbalance, such as the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (ROC AUC). In this work, we study the correlation of the spelling rate with 13 metrics to identify which among them best reflect user spelling performance and how they are affected by trial repetition. The Results of two datasets (a private LARESI ERP dataset and the public OpenBMI ERP dataset) favor the Brier score, Matthews Correlation Coefficient (MCC), and the metrics that account for class imbalance in binary classification: ROC AUC, area under the Precision-Recall curve (PR AUC), Average Precision (AP), and partial AUC (pAUC). These findings encourage researchers and practitioners to report those metrics in ERP-based BCI experiments.

</details>


### [92] [Valdi: Value Diffusion World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00917)
*Christopher Lindenberg,Kashyap Chitta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Value Diffusion World Models (Valdi)，将端到端在线训练MPC与潜在扩散动力学模型结合，在CarRacing环境中使用单步扩散即可达到确定性MLP基线的性能，并揭示了预测多模态性与控制性能之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型可以实现模型预测控制，但需要同时满足在线使用的快速性和表达不确定未来的表现力。扩散模型天然适合建模不确定动力学，但其迭代推理过程难以用于低延迟的潜在规划。现有方法存在速度与表现力之间的鸿沟。

Method: Valdi将潜在扩散动力学模型与端到端在线MPC训练相结合，在训练和推理时仅使用单步扩散步骤，以平衡速度与表现力。

Result: 在CarRacing环境中的初步实验表明，Valdi使用单步扩散即可匹配确定性MLP基线的性能。实验揭示了预测多模态性与控制性能之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: Valdi成功弥合了扩散模型在低延迟潜在规划中的应用差距，但多模态预测与下游控制性能的权衡仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: World models can enable Model Predictive Control (MPC), but this requires dynamics prediction that is both fast enough for online use and expressive enough to represent uncertain futures. Diffusion models offer a natural mechanism for modeling uncertain dynamics, yet their iterative inference procedure makes them difficult to use for low-latency latent planning. We bridge this gap with Value Diffusion World Models (Valdi), combining end-to-end online training for MPC with a latent diffusion dynamics model. In preliminary experiments on the CarRacing environment, we show that Valdi, using a single diffusion step at both training and inference, matches a deterministic MLP baseline. Our experiments expose a trade-off between predictive multimodality and control performance in this setup. Code is available at https://github.com/Kit115/ValueDiffusionWorldModels.

</details>


### [93] [Task-Relevant Representation Decoupling for Visual Reinforcement Learning Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00796)
*Jinwen Wang,Youfang Lin,Xiaobo Hu,Qian Xu,Shuo Wang,Zhuo Chen,Kai Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督的任务相关表征解耦算法（T2RD），通过将观测分解为任务相关和任务无关的表征，并结合一致性约束、交叉重建和交叉动态预测三个组件，显著提升了视觉强化学习在控制任务中的泛化性能和样本效率，在DeepMind Control Suite和机器人操作任务上取得了SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉强化学习在控制任务中取得了成功，但学到的策略泛化到新环境时存在严重过拟合问题，智能体往往过度关注训练环境中与任务无关的特征，导致泛化性能差。

Method: 提出T2RD算法，包含三个组件：1) 任务相关表征一致性（task-relevant representation consistency）；2) 交叉重建（cross-reconstruction）；3) 交叉动态预测（cross-dynamic prediction）。前两个组件实现内容与风格特征的解耦，第三个组件通过引入动态预测从内容表征中进一步提炼任务相关特征。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite和机器人操作任务上，T2RD在泛化性能和样本效率方面均达到了当前最优（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: 通过自监督方式解耦任务相关与任务无关表征，可以有效提升VRL的泛化能力，其中动态预测是确保解耦出的内容特征真正与任务相关的关键组件。

Abstract: Visual Reinforcement Learning (VRL) has achieved considerable success in solving control tasks. However, generalizing learned policies to new environments remains a major challenge, as agents often overfit to task-irrelevant features in the training environment. To solve this problem, we introduce the concept of decoupling observations into task-relevant and task-irrelevant representations. Building on this idea, we propose a self-supervised Task-Relevant Representation Decoupling (T2RD) algorithm for VRL. This algorithm consists of three components: task-relevant representation consistency, cross-reconstruction, and cross-dynamic prediction. The first two components achieve the decoupling of content and style features, but the resulting content representations are not necessarily task-relevant. To further refine task-relevant features from content representations, we design the third component that introduces dynamic prediction. T2RD achieves State-Of-The-Art (SOTA) generalization performance and sample efficiency in the DeepMind Control Suite and Robotic Manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [94] [Human-Machine Collaboration on Generative Meta-Learning: Model and Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00926)
*Midhun Parakkal Unni,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合人类反馈的生成式元学习框架(GMHF)，利用专家直觉指导数据合成，弥合训练域与目标域之间的分布差异，理论证明和实验验证均表明该方法能有效提升模型在分布偏移下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在面对与训练分布不同的环境时泛化能力不足，尤其是当目标域数据完全或部分不可用时，现有方法难以有效弥合这一领域鸿沟。

Method: 提出GMHF框架：以条件神经常微分方程(cNODE)作为生成式数字孪生，结合强化学习(RL)智能体根据人类专家反馈迭代优化生成轨迹的潜在物理参数，引导元学习器朝向未观察到的目标分布迁移。

Result: 在非线性Duffing振子实验中，GMHF显著降低了部署损失，且专家可靠性越高效果越好；生成数据与目标数据之间的散度在可靠反馈下显著下降，验证了理论预测的散度最小化机制。在非动力学的概率模型上同样验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 理论证明了将生成数据分布与人类对目标物理的信念对齐可显著降低泛化误差；实验表明人机协作可作为分布偏移下鲁棒泛化的严谨催化剂。

Abstract: Generalizing machine learning models to environments that differ from their training distribution remains a critical hurdle, particularly when data from the target domain is entirely or partially unavailable. We propose Generative Meta-Learning with Human Feedback (GMHF), a novel framework that bridges this domain gap by leveraging expert intuition to guide data synthesis. Grounded in a theoretical analysis of generalization error, we derive bounds demonstrating that aligning the distribution of generated data with human beliefs regarding the target physics significantly mitigates risk. GMHF operationalizes this insight by employing a Conditional Neural ODE (cNODE) as a generative digital twin, coupled with a Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent. The agent iteratively refines the latent physical parameters of the generated trajectories based on feedback, effectively steering the meta-learner toward the unobserved target distribution. Empirical validation on a nonlinear Duffing oscillator shows that GMHF substantially reduces deployment loss as expert reliability increases, and that the divergence between generated and target data falls under reliable feedback, directly corroborating the divergence-minimisation mechanism predicted by our theory. Further experiments on a non-dynamical probabilistic model confirm that the framework extends beyond ODE-governed systems, establishing human-AI collaboration as a rigorous catalyst for robust generalisation under distribution shift.

</details>


### [95] [Local Motion Matters: A Deconstruct-Recompose Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning Pre-training from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00808)
*Jinwen Wang,Youfang Lin,Xiaobo Hu,Shuo Wang,Kai Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种解构-重组范式(DRP)，通过将全局运动解构为局部原子动作并学习其可迁移表示，再重组为整体运动表征，实现了跨形态的强化学习迁移，在多种机器人控制任务中显著提升了样本效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频预训练的强化学习方法通常将智能体视为不可分割的整体进行全局运动建模，这种全局建模与形态紧密耦合，阻碍了跨领域迁移。然而，尽管全局运动差异巨大，不同智能体的局部组件却表现出相似的运动模式，因此需要一种能够学习可迁移局部运动表示的方法。

Method: 提出解构-重组范式(DRP)，包含两个阶段：(1) 解构阶段：识别多个局部点并跟踪其帧间运动，定义为原子动作(Atomic Action)，并引入双注意力编码器(DAE)学习局部运动表示及其时空关系；(2) 重组阶段：通过可学习的运动聚合标记[MAT]在潜动力学模型中将局部运动表示重组为整体运动表示，并使用适配器桥接局部运动与下游动作特定动力学以加速策略学习。

Result: 该方法有效迁移到多种机器人控制和操作任务中，显著提升了样本效率和任务性能。

Conclusion: 通过对运动进行解构-重组，学习与形态解耦的局部运动表示，能够有效实现跨领域的强化学习迁移，为大规模视频预训练提升强化学习效率提供了新范式。

Abstract: Pre-training on large-scale videos to improve reinforcement learning efficiency is promising yet remains challenging. Existing methods typically treat the agent as an indivisible entity, modeling motion patterns globally. Such global modeling is tightly coupled with the morphology, hindering transfer across domains. In contrast, despite the vast disparity in global motions, the local components exhibit similar motion patterns across different agents. Building on this insight, we propose a novel Deconstruct-Recompose Paradigm (DRP) for learning transferable local motion representations. Specifically, in the Deconstruct phase, we identify multiple local points and track their frame-wise motions, defining each as an Atomic Action. We introduce a Dual-Attention Encoder (DAE) to learn local motion representations from these Atomic Actions, capturing their spatiotemporal relationships. In the Recompose phase, we compose local motion representations with a learnable Motion Aggregation Token [MAT] via latent dynamics model learning. Additionally, an adapter bridges local motion and downstream action-specific dynamics to accelerate policy learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively transfers to diverse robotic control and manipulation tasks, significantly improving sample efficiency and performance.

</details>


### [96] [Aionoscope: Debugging Latent-State Accessibility in Time-Series Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00956)
*Alexander Chemeris,Ming Jin,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Aionoscope是一个基于生成器的诊断工具，用于调试冻结时间序列表示中的潜在状态可访问性。通过对37个模型+适配器系统的评估，发现大多数系统能很好地恢复组件存在性，但密集过程状态的可靠暴露程度较低（最高密集探针行均值掩码R²为0.689，而密集特征oracle达到0.999），揭示了粗粒度和细粒度可访问性之间的不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型评估仅关注预测或分类性能，无法反映表示是否保留了用户可能需要检查的过程状态（如事件时序、相位、振幅、频率或状态变量），因此需要一种能够调试潜在状态可访问性的诊断工具。

Method: 提出Aionoscope，将过程生成与观测渲染分离，生成带有精确类别和密集标签的种子合成流，涵盖混合复杂性和干扰变化。实例化为原始过程混合（Primitive Process Mixtures），使用统一的线性探针协议评估37个模型+适配器系统。

Result: 大多数系统能轻松恢复组件存在性，但暴露密集过程状态的可靠性较低：最高密集探针行均值掩码R²为0.689，而密集特征oracle达到0.999。

Conclusion: 粗粒度与细粒度可访问性之间存在不匹配：表示在"存在什么类型的信号"层面看似信息丰富，但隐藏了调试所需的时间、相位、振幅、频率或状态变量。Aionoscope能够揭示这一失败模式。

Abstract: Time-series models are often evaluated by what they can forecast or classify, but those scores do not show whether their representations preserve the process state a user may want to inspect: event timing, phase, amplitude, frequency, or regime variables. We introduce Aionoscope, a generator-based diagnostic tool for debugging latent-state accessibility in frozen time-series representations. Aionoscope separates process generation from observation rendering, producing seeded synthetic streams with exact categorical and dense labels across mixture complexity and nuisance variation.
  We instantiate Aionoscope as Primitive Process Mixtures and evaluate 37 model-plus-adapter systems with a common pooled linear-probe protocol. The main result is a mismatch between coarse and fine-grained accessibility. Most systems make component presence easy to recover, but expose dense process state much less reliably: the highest observed dense-probe row reaches 0.689 mean masked $R^2$, while a dense-feature oracle reaches 0.999. This is the failure mode Aionoscope is designed to surface: a representation can look informative at the level of "what kind of signal is present" while hiding the timing, phase, amplitude, frequency, or regime variables needed for debugging.

</details>


### [97] [From Pixels to Temporal Correlations: Learning Informative Representations for Reinforcement Learning Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00811)
*Jinwen Wang,Youfang Lin,Xiaobo Hu,Siyu Yang,Sheng Han,Shuo Wang,Kai Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多尺度时序对比学习（MTCL）方法，通过建模多尺度时序相关性来平衡对视频中各元素的关注，从而学习更丰富的表征，提升下游强化学习任务的样本效率和渐近性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督视频预训练方法主要依赖单步转移预测和图像重建，但这类方法倾向于保留像素空间中占比大的静态信息，忽视了占比小但关键的信息，导致表征信息量不足。

Method: 提出时间关联空间来区分每个元素，并引入多尺度时序对比学习（MTCL）方法，分别建模多尺度的时间相关性，从而平衡不同元素的注意力，生成信息更丰富的表征。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多个下游任务中提升了样本效率和渐近性能。

Conclusion: 通过多尺度时序对比学习能够更充分地利用大规模无标签视频数据，获得更丰富的表征，有效支持下游策略学习。

Abstract: Unsupervised pre-training on large-scale datasets has demonstrated significant potential for improving the sample efficiency and performance of Reinforcement Learning (RL). Given the large-scale action-free internet videos, existing methods utilize single-step transition prediction and image reconstruction to learn representations. However, these methods prefer to preserve large-proportion stationary information in the pixel space, neglecting small but crucial information. To preserve enough information in the representation, it is essential to pay equal attention to each element in videos. Specifically, we propose a temporal correlation space to distinguish each element. For implementation, we introduce the Multi-scale Temporal Contrastive Learning (MTCL) method to model multi-scale temporal correlations separately. This approach can balance the attention of different elements and yield more informative representations, effectively supporting policy learning in various downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that our method improves sample efficiency and asymptotic performance across various downstream tasks.

</details>


### [98] [Staleness-Learning Rate Scaling Laws for Asynchronous RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01083)
*Jingwei Song,Haofeng Xu,Jie Xiao,Chengke Bao,Jingwei Shi,Pengbin Feng,Weixun Wang,Yuhang Han,Chuan Wu,Linfeng Zhang,Bill Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究异步GRPO中策略梯度的过时性（stale rollouts）问题，证明每步梯度偏差为O(S·η)，并推导出稳定性条件依赖于S·η和T·η的双约束缩放律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高吞吐量RLHF系统常将rollout生成与策略优化解耦，导致学习器更新时使用过时的rollout。这种过时性对异步GRPO的影响尚不明确，本文旨在理论分析其偏差与稳定性边界。

Method: 将行为策略显式引入GRPO替代目标，区分替代梯度映射与真实总导数；在局部有界性、分布平滑性和行为策略平滑性假设下，推导过时rollout带来的梯度偏差阶数；进一步推导条件性坍塌时间缩放律，分析累积漂移与过时约束对稳定性的影响。

Result: 1) 每步替代梯度偏差为O(S·η)，S为最大rollout滞后，η为学习率；2) 条件性坍塌时间遵循双约束稳定性条件：η << min{R_batch / (S·G_upd), R_crit / (T·G_upd)}，解释了在有限视界下最大稳定学习率为何可能对过时性呈现弱依赖。

Conclusion: 过时rollout引入的偏差在累积漂移低于裁剪半径时主要由T·η主导，而过时约束激活时稳定性显式依赖于S·η。两约束条件共同决定了异步GRPO的稳定学习率上限。

Abstract: High-throughput RLHF systems often decouple rollout generation from policy optimization, leading to the use of stale rollouts during learner updates. In this work, we study the effect of such staleness in asynchronous GRPO. We make the behavior policy explicit in the GRPO surrogate objective and distinguish between the surrogate-gradient mapping used by the learner and the true total derivative of a distribution-dependent population objective. Under assumptions of local boundedness, distributional smoothness, and behavior-policy smoothness, we show that stale rollouts introduce a per-step surrogate-gradient bias of order O(S * eta), where S denotes the maximum rollout lag and eta denotes the learning rate. We further derive a conditional collapse-time scaling law: when within-cycle drift remains below a batch-level clipping radius, collapse is governed primarily by cumulative learner drift T * eta; when the stale-rollout constraint is active, stability instead depends explicitly on S * eta. This yields a two-constraint stability condition eta << min{R_batch / (S * G_upd), R_crit / (T * G_upd)}, explaining why the maximum stable learning rate may appear weakly dependent on staleness in the horizon-limited regime.

</details>


### [99] [Spectroscopy Analysis with Machine Learning Regression for the Quantification of Carbon and Nitrogen Contents in Inceptisol and Oxisol Soil Types: Comparing Different Preprocessing and Validation methods as well as Feature Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00834)
*Vinicius Herique Kieling,Guilherme Macedo Baggio,Felipe Augusto Bueno Rossi,Marco Antonio de Castro Barbosa,Dalcimar Casanova,Larissa Macedo dos Santos Tonial,Jefferson Tales Oliva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于近红外光谱和堆叠集成机器学习的便携式土壤碳氮含量快速预测方法，在氧化土和始成土上取得了RPD>2.0的良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统土壤分析方法耗时、成本高，而近红外光谱技术具备快速、低成本、无损检测的优势，需要开发高效的预测模型以实现土壤碳氮的快速定量。

Method: 使用便携式MyNIR设备采集近红外光谱数据，采用Savitzky-Golay滤波和基于NIPALS-Huber损失的稳健离群值去除方法进行预处理；以PLS、SVR、Ridge为基模型，线性回归为元模型的堆叠集成学习进行建模，采用10折交叉验证、留一法和Kennard-Stone留出法等多种验证策略。

Result: 模型RPD>2.0，过拟合程度低；不同土壤类型（氧化土与始成土）之间存在性能差异，表明土壤学特性对预测有影响。

Conclusion: 该方法可实现对土壤碳氮含量的快速准确量化，有助于优化可持续农业实践，支持基于有机质含量和养分可用性的快速决策。

Abstract: Near-Infrared (NIR) spectroscopy has emerged as a promising alternative to traditional soil analysis methods, offering advantages such as speed, low cost, and non-destructive testing. This work proposes a machine learning (ML) approach to calibrate predictive models for carbon (C) and nitrogen (N) content in Oxisols and Inceptisols, utilizing NIR spectral data acquired with a portable MyNIR device. Various preprocessing methods were evaluated, with the most effective being the Savitzky-Golay (SG) filter and a robust outlier removal method based on the Nonlinear Iterative Partial Least Squares (NIPALS) algorithm coupled with a Huber loss function. Multiple validation strategies were compared, including 10-fold cross-validation, leave-one-out, and holdout via the Kennard-Stone method, followed by standardization. Stacking ensemble learning models were employed, using Partial Least Squares (PLS), Support Vector Regression (SVR), and Ridge as base models, with linear regression as the meta-model. The models were evaluated using R2, Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE), Mean Absolute Error (MAE), and Ratio of Performance Deviation (RPD) metrics. The performance gap between soil types suggests the influence of pedological characteristics. Furthermore, the models achieved an RPD > 2.0 with low overfitting, validating the potential of this approach for rapid C and N quantification. This study contributes to the optimization of sustainable agricultural practices, aligning with the demand for efficient and environmentally friendly analytical methods. The developed technique enables faster decision-making for producers and consultants based on organic matter content, fertility indicators, and nutrient availability.

</details>


### [100] [CausalMix: Data Mixture as Causal Inference for Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01104)
*Zinan Tang,Yukun Zhang,Shaomian Zheng,Zhuoshi Pan,Qizhi Pei,Dingnan Jin,Jun Zhou,Yujun Wang,Biqing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalMix，将数据混合优化建模为因果推断问题，通过估计条件平均处理效应（CATE）来动态推断最优数据混合比例，克服了传统方法依赖静态数据分布、无法扩展的局限。实验表明CausalMix在多种下游任务上优于RegMix等基线方法，并提供了可解释的混合策略可视化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据混合优化方法（如RegMix）通过代理模型优化混合权重，但假设数据分布是静态的。当数据池发生变化时，这些方法需要从头重新训练，无法从小规模设置无缝扩展到更大的数据池和模型规模。作者希望解决这一扩展性问题。

Method: 将数据混合优化建模为因果推断问题：以数据池的统计特征作为协变量，领域混合作为处理变量。在512次Qwen2.5-0.5B运行上拟合因果模型估计条件平均处理效应（CATE），然后外推得到800K数据池的最优混合比例，并用于训练7B模型。该方法还成功泛化到Qwen3-4B-Base的长思维链数据上。

Result: CausalMix引导的混合策略在多种下游任务上持续提升性能，优于RegMix及其他基线方法。同时通过CATE解释器提供了混合策略的可视化分析。

Conclusion: CausalMix提供了一个因果且可解释的框架用于优化LLM数据混合，能够动态推断状态依赖的最优数据混合比例，解决了传统方法无法扩展的问题。

Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) training, data mixing plays a pivotal role in determining model performance. Recent methods optimize mixture weights via proxy models, but they rely on the assumption of static data distributions. As a result, when the underlying data pool shifts, these methods require costly retraining from scratch. This limitation restricts their ability to scale seamlessly from small settings to larger data pools and model sizes. In this paper, we propose CausalMix to address this limitation by casting data mixture optimization as a causal inference problem. We formulate the statistical features of the data pool as covariates and the domain mixture as the treatment. After fitting a causal model on 512 runs of Qwen2.5-0.5B to estimate the Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE), we extrapolate the optimal mixture for an 800K data pool and apply it to train a 7B model. Furthermore, we successfully generalize the framework to long chain-of-thought data on Qwen3-4B-Base. By leveraging causal modeling to isolate confounding biases, CausalMix dynamically infers state-dependent optimal data mixtures. Extensive experiments show that the mixture guided by CausalMix consistently improves performance across multiple downstream tasks, outperforming RegMix and other baselines. In addition, we use the CATE Interpreter to provide visual analysis of the learned mixing strategy. Overall, CausalMix offers a causal and interpretable framework for optimizing LLM data mixtures.

</details>


### [101] [Constrained Bayesian Optimisation with Multiple Information Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00865)
*Hauke Maathuis,Roeland De Breuker,Saullo Castro,Maike Osborne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多源贝叶斯优化框架，通过整合辅助数据源（如代理模型或简化仿真）来解决可行域极小情况下的约束优化问题，扩展了约束最大熵搜索方法，在合成和物理基准测试中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可行域很小的约束优化问题中，仅依赖真实目标函数和约束的评估难以高效探索设计空间，而现有方法大多忽视了辅助数据源（如代理模型或简化仿真）在早期探索中的潜力。

Method: 提出多源约束最大熵搜索（Multi-source Constrained Max-value Entropy Search）框架，建模不同数据源之间的相关性，同时平衡评估成本与信息增益。

Result: 在合成和物理基准测试中，该方法即使在辅助数据弱相关的情况下也能有效识别可行且最优的解，在早期探索阶段持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 整合多源辅助信息是提升约束贝叶斯优化在可行域极小场景下性能的有效途径，所提方法具有良好的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimisation (BO) under unknown constraints is particularly challenging when feasible regions are small. In such settings, existing methods that typically rely solely on evaluations of the true objective and constraints struggle to efficiently explore the design space. However, many real-world applications offer auxiliary data sources (e.g. surrogate models or simplified simulations) that can support early exploration. Despite this potential, their integration into constrained BO remains largely unexplored. We propose a general multi-source framework that extends constrained Max-value Entropy Search, capturing inter-source correlation while balancing evaluation cost and information gain. Experiments on both synthetic and physics-based benchmarks show that our method efficiently identifies feasible and optimal solutions, even when auxiliary data are only weakly correlated. The proposed approach consistently outperforms existing methods, particularly in early-stage exploration.

</details>


### [102] [Muon as a Residual Connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01124)
*Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon优化器可被理解为一种隐式的残差连接，它通过正交化更新牺牲局部梯度保真度来改善下游层的表征保留，在训练大型神经网络中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon作为训练大型神经网络的有效优化器已取得实证成功，但其成功机制尚缺乏统一解释，现有研究从多个不同角度进行阐述，作者希望提供一种简洁的机制性理解。

Method: 提出将Muon视为隐式残差连接的机制解释：正交化更新会牺牲部分即时梯度保真度，但能改善下游层的表征保留。在线性优化设定下研究这一权衡。

Result: 在线性优化设定中，Muon能够学习到对局部目标拟合较慢但更易于下游层利用的表征。

Conclusion: 为Muon提供了概念性解释，并为设计平衡局部下降与下游可用性的优化器提供了设计视角。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as one of the most effective optimizers for training large neural networks, yet its empirical success has been explained from several different perspectives. In this paper, we propose a simple mechanistic interpretation: Muon can be understood as an implicit residual connection during training. Specifically, orthogonalizing the update can sacrifice some immediate gradient fidelity while improving representation preservation for downstream layers. We study this trade-off in controlled linear optimization settings, where Muon can learn representations that are slower to fit a local target but easier for downstream layers to exploit. Our results suggest a conceptual explanation for Muon and a design perspective for optimizers that balance local descent with downstream usability.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond Activation Alignment:The Alignment-Diversity Tradeoff in Task-Aware LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00908)
*Fei Wang,Chao Xue,Taoran Liu,Li Shen,Ye Liu,ChangXing Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现两个关键现象：困惑度错觉（基于困惑度的层重要性排序与复杂推理任务表现几乎不相关，Kendall τ≈0）和对齐-多样性权衡（仅用目标任务校准数据会损害量化性能，而加入通用域数据可提升鲁棒性）。基于此提出TASA框架，通过训练无关的梯度迹对齐准则搜索最优校准数据混合，并聚合困惑度与推理导向的敏感度信号指导混合精度位分配。实验表明，适当分配的3.5位模型可匹配或超越4位基线，在GSM8K上提升超20个绝对百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示现有混合精度量化方法中基于困惑度的敏感度评估与复杂推理性能之间的严重不一致性，以及校准数据组成对量化效果的关键影响。

Method: 提出TASA双层框架：外层通过梯度迹对齐准则搜索最优校准数据混合比例；内层聚合困惑度与推理导向敏感度信号，指导层间和层内混合精度位分配。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上，3.5位平均精度下TASA匹配或超越多个4位均匀量化基线，在GSM8K上比最强W3基线提升超过20个绝对百分点。

Conclusion: 校准数据组成对任务感知量化有显著影响，这一因素在先前工作中未被充分探索；合理的混合精度分配可在更低比特率下实现更优性能。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization (MPQ) has become a key technique for deploying large language models under stringent memory and compute constraints. We first identify a phenomenon that we term the Perplexity Illusion: layers ranked as important by perplexity-based sensitivity show little rank correlation with those that are most influential for complex reasoning performance, with Kendall $τ\approx 0$ in our analysis. We further reveal an Alignment-Diversity Tradeoff: using only target-task calibration data can degrade post-quantization performance, whereas incorporating general-domain data stabilizes sensitivity estimation and improves robustness across tasks. Based on these observations, we propose TASA (Task-Aware Sensitivity Analysis), a two-level framework that jointly optimizes calibration-data composition and mixed-precision bit allocation. Specifically, TASA searches for a calibration-data mixture using a training-free gradient-trace alignment criterion, and then aggregates perplexity and reasoning-oriented sensitivity signals to guide both inter-layer and intra-layer bit allocation. Experiments on LLaMA-3-8B and Qwen2.5-7B reveal a precision inversion: appropriately allocated 3.5-bit models can match or surpass less task-aware 4-bit baselines. At an average precision of 3.5 bits, TASA matches or outperforms several competitive 4-bit uniform baselines in aggregate accuracy, and improves over the strongest W3 baseline on GSM8K by more than 20 absolute points on LLaMA-3-8B. These results show that calibration-data composition substantially affects task-sensitive quantization, a factor underexplored in prior work.

</details>


### [104] [Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps for Online Feedback-Driven Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01144)
*Binglin Ji,Anindya Sarkar,Hengchang Lu,Jens Sjölund,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IMPFM是一种基于序贯控制的多粒子交互流映射框架，通过后验样本共享和探索-利用重加权机制，实现样本高效的在线反馈驱动搜索，在偏好未知的全局探索任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在奖励对齐中擅长局部探索，但在偏好未知、仅通过序贯反馈逐步揭示的场景下缺乏广域探索能力，难以发现高效用区域。

Method: 提出IMPFM框架：1) 基于流映射的多粒子后验样本共享机制，在重采样步骤中用集体后验样本校正单个粒子漂移；2) 探索-利用重加权机制，通过多粒子交互保持结构多样性并克服SMC采样的权重退化问题；3) 证明该采样框架产生多粒子交互感知的Feynman-Kac校正器，使系统渐进收敛到KL倾斜目标分布。

Result: 在多种搜索和对齐任务上的大量经验评估和消融实验证实，IMPFM在样本效率和全局探索能力上显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: IMPFM通过序贯校正的多粒子动力学，显式保持结构多样性，有效缓解奖励过度优化问题，为异质偏好对齐提供了全局探索的解决方案。

Abstract: While generative models have enabled training-free reward alignment, current methods typically excel in local exploration within narrow regions of the underlying distribution. These approaches struggle when preferences are unknown a priori and only revealed through sequential feedback-a scenario demanding broad exploration to uncover high-utility regions. To address this, we propose Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps (IMPFM), a framework for sample-efficient online feedback-driven search. IMPFM progressively transports a group of interactive particles toward the target distribution, maintaining the broad coverage essential for heterogeneous preference alignment. IMPFM introduces a principled and efficient posterior sample sharing mechanism across particles powered by flow maps. By correcting individual particle drift with the collective posterior samples of the entire ensemble at each resampling step, the framework maximizes sample utility to enable global exploration while actively mitigating reward over-optimization, typical of standard control frameworks. Paired with a principled exploration-exploitation reweighting mechanism involving multi-particle interaction, this sequentially corrected multi-particle dynamics explicitly preserves structural diversity and overcomes the weight degeneracy inherent to standard SMC samplers. Crucially, we prove that the resulting sampling framework yields a multi-particle interaction-aware Feynman-Kac corrector that progressively steers the multi-particle system toward a KL-tilted target distribution, facilitating global exploration and preventing mode collapse. Extensive empirical evaluations and rigorous ablations across diverse search and alignment tasks confirm the efficacy of IMPFM over existing baselines.

</details>


### [105] [Right in the Right Way: LM Training with Verifiable Rewards and Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01181)
*Mehul Damani,Isha Puri,Idan Shenfeld,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗式生成器-判别器框架，将可验证奖励与人类演示中学习到的信号相结合，在保持RLVR准确性的同时，改善生成文本的非可验证属性（如风格和多样性），有效缓解多样性崩溃、奖励作弊等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR方法仅优化可客观评分的指标，忽略了主观、不可验证的人类输出属性（如风格和结构），导致多样性崩溃、回答不自然和奖励作弊等问题。

Method: 提出对抗式生成器-判别器框架。生成器通过RL训练最大化任务准确性和来自判别器的对抗奖励；判别器与生成器策略联合训练，学习区分人类输出与模型生成输出，作为人类输出分布的代理提供难以形式化为标量奖励的反馈。

Result: 在bug修复任务中显著降低编辑距离；在故事生成中提升胜率并生成更多样化、更类人的故事；在奖励作弊基准中几乎消除模型异常行为，同时保持高分。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了RL和SFT之间的差距，提供了一条可扩展的路径，能够联合优化任务的可验证和非可验证属性。

Abstract: RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for training LMs on tasks with well-defined success metrics, such as code generation and mathematical reasoning. However, current RLVR methods optimize only what can be objectively scored, often neglecting subjective, non-verifiable aspects of human-like outputs, such as style and structure. This limitation leads to well-documented failure modes such as diversity collapse, unnatural-sounding responses, and reward hacking. We propose an adversarial generator-discriminator framework that augments verifiable rewards with a learned signal from human demonstrations. A generator model is trained using RL to maximize both task accuracy and an adversarial reward derived from a discriminator. The discriminator, trained alongside the generator policy, learns to distinguish human-written outputs from model-generated ones. The discriminator serves as a learned proxy for the human output distribution, providing feedback on aspects of generation that are difficult to formalize as scalar rewards. Across diverse domains, including bug fixing and open-ended generation, our approach consistently improves non-verifiable properties while preserving the accuracy gains of RLVR. In bug fixing, our method produces solutions with significantly lower edit distance compared to RLVR baselines while matching end performance. In story generation, our method significantly improves win rate while producing stories that are diverse and more human-like. And in a simple reward hacking benchmark, our method nearly eliminates model misbehavior while maintaining high benchmark scores. Together, these results show that our approach bridges RL and SFT, offering a scalable path toward jointly optimizing the verifiable and non-verifiable properties of a task.

</details>


### [106] [Language-Critique Imitation Learning from Suboptimal Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01225)
*Chih-Han Yang,Dai-Jie Wu,Yun-Ping Huang,Ping-Chun Hsieh,Kenneth Marino,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语言 critique 的模仿学习框架，利用自然语言作为结构化监督信号替代传统标量信号，从次优演示中学习鲁棒策略。方法包括构建语言标签描述进度、识别次优行为并提供细粒度纠正指导，以及提出语言-critique损失函数（LC-BC和LC-DP）。理论证明该目标在标准假设下是专家性能差距的上界。实验在导航、操作和游戏等连续控制任务中超过强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于标量信号（置信度、判别器分数、重要性权重）的次优演示模仿学习存在信息压缩损失，无法表达中间推理、失败模式和纠正行为等结构化反馈，限制了策略学习质量。

Method: (1) 构建语言标签：从次优演示中生成显式描述当前进度、识别次优行为并提供细粒度纠正指导的自然语言标注；(2) 提出语言-critique损失函数，直接利用结构化语言信号训练策略，避免压缩为标量；(3) 分别实例化为行为克隆（LC-BC）和扩散策略（LC-DP）；(4) 提供理论分析证明所提目标函数是专家性能差距的上界。

Result: 在导航、操作和游戏等多种连续控制任务上，LC-BC和LC-DP均一致优于强模仿学习和离线强化学习基线方法，验证了语言作为结构化监督信号的有效性。

Conclusion: 自然语言可以作为次优演示模仿学习中强大的结构化监督信号，通过显式表达推理和纠正信息避免标量压缩带来的信息损失，从而学习更鲁棒的控制策略。

Abstract: Prior work on imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations typically relies on compressed supervision signals such as confidence estimates, discriminator scores, or importance weights. These scalar signals are inherently limited, as they cannot explicitly express intermediate reasoning about task progress, failure modes, or corrective actions. We propose a language-critique framework for imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations that instead leverages natural language as a structured supervision signal, avoiding the collapse of expressive feedback into scalars. Our method first constructs language labels from demonstrations that explicitly describe current progress, identify suboptimal behaviors, and provide fine-grained corrective guidance. We then introduce a language-critique loss that directly trains policies using these structured signals without reducing them to scalars, and instantiate it for both behavior cloning and diffusion policies, yielding LC-BC and LC-DP. We further provide a theoretical result showing that the proposed objective upper-bounds the expert performance gap under standard assumptions. Empirically, we evaluate on diverse continuous control tasks spanning navigation, manipulation, and gameplay, where our methods consistently outperform strong imitation learning and offline reinforcement learning baselines. These results demonstrate that language can serve as a powerful and structured form of supervision for learning robust policies from suboptimal data.

</details>


### [107] [Explainable AI for Cancer Drug Response Prediction: Beyond Univariate Feature Attributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00931)
*Martino Ciaperoni,Margherita Lalli,Simone Piaggesi,Martina Varisco,Francesco Carli,Riccardo Guidotti,Dino Pedreschi,Francesco Raimondi,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ILLUME+是一个可扩展的事后可解释性框架，超越单基因评估，通过多形式解释捕捉协调的基因活动，在癌症药物反应预测中产生更稳定的基因重要性分数，恢复已知药物-基因关联和机制，并支持AI辅助假设生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习模型在预测癌症药物反应时，可解释性方法计算成本高、缺乏鲁棒性，且将复杂的药物响应简化为单基因重要性评分，忽略了驱动敏感性和耐药性的协调基因活动。

Method: ILLUME+是一个可扩展的事后可解释性框架，集成到端到端流程中，捕获多种互补形式解释，超越单基因评估。

Result: ILLUME+比现有基线方法产生更稳定的基因重要性分数，恢复已建立的药物-基因关联和作用机制，并能够发现新型交互驱动的分子信号。

Conclusion: ILLUME+不仅提高了可解释性的稳定性和鲁棒性，还通过捕捉协调基因活动实现了AI辅助假设生成，为精准肿瘤学中的生物发现提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Predicting cancer drug response from transcriptomic profiles is a cornerstone of precision oncology, yet the scientific value of machine learning models hinges not solely on predictive accuracy, but also on their capacity to generate reliable biological insights. Current explainability approaches in this setting are computationally costly, lack robustness, and reduce complex drug response to univariate gene importance scores, overlooking the coordinated gene activity that drives sensitivity and resistance. In this work, we present ILLUME+, a scalable post-hoc explainability framework that moves beyond single-gene assessments to capture multiple, complementary forms of explanation. Integrated into our end-to-end pipeline, ILLUME+ produces more stable gene importance scores than existing baselines, recovers established drug-gene associations and mechanisms of action, and enables AI-assisted hypothesis generation to uncover novel interaction-driven molecular signals in cancer biology.

</details>


### [108] [Diffeomorphic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00947)
*Ludwig Winkler,Andrew Leaver-Fay,Joseph Kleinhenz,Pan Kessel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出微分同胚优化方法，将流模型从数据流形映射到简单基空间进行梯度下降，等价于黎曼梯度下降（O(λ²)修正），并扩展到SO(3)和SE(3)矩阵李群。在蛋白质设计中，该方法在二级结构靶向（91.3% vs 63.3%）、肽结合亲和力（2倍速度优于OC-Flow）和Rosetta能量降低方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型学习的数据分布位于高维空间中的低维流形上，在该流形上优化可微目标函数面临挑战：环境损失景观是高维、崎岖且非凸的，直接梯度下降会忽略流形几何结构而迅速偏离流形。

Method: 利用扩散模型和流模型提供从数据流形到更简单基空间的映射，在基空间执行梯度下降。通过微分几何证明，该方法等价于数据流形上的黎曼梯度下降（O(λ²)修正），保证轨迹始终位于流形上。进一步扩展到SO(3)和SE(3)矩阵李群，推导了可自动微分的SO(3)梯度，以及用于通过李群ODE求解器反向传播的广义伴随状态方法。

Result: 在二级结构靶向任务中，结合FrameFlow达到91.3%残基在Ramachandran靶向区域内（对比63.3%）；在肽结合亲和力任务中以2倍速度优于OC-Flow；在PDB测试集上，对于数百残基的结构，Rosetta能量降低数千单位。

Conclusion: 微分同胚优化通过在简单基空间执行梯度下降避免直接在高维流形上优化，提供更平滑的优化曲面，显著提升了蛋白质设计任务性能，且可扩展到李群上的优化问题。

Abstract: Generative models learn data distributions that reside on a low-dimensional manifold within a higher-dimensional ambient space. Optimizing differentiable objectives on this manifold is challenging: the ambient loss landscape is high-dimensional, rugged, and non-convex. Direct gradient descent, blind to the manifold's geometry, quickly drifts off it. Diffeomorphic optimization starts from the observation that diffusion and flow models provide a map from the data manifold to a much simpler base space in which we perform gradient descent. Using differential geometry, we show this is equivalent to Riemannian gradient descent on the data manifold up to $\mathcal{O}(λ^2)$ corrections, keeping trajectories on-manifold by construction and yielding a smoother optimization surface. For protein design, we extend diffeomorphic optimization to the matrix Lie groups $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ and $\mathrm{SE}(3)$, deriving an autograd-compatible $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ gradient and a generalized adjoint-state method for backpropagation through Lie-group ODE solvers. Diffeomorphic optimization improves over tuned guidance on secondary-structure targeting with FrameFlow ($91.3\%$ vs. $63.3\%$ of residues in the Ramachandran target), outperforms OC-Flow on peptide binding affinity at $2\times$ the speed, and reduces Rosetta energies by thousands of units across the PDB test set for structures with hundreds of residues.

</details>


### [109] [LeNEPA: No-Augmentation Next-Latent Prediction for Time-Series Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00958)
*Alexander Chemeris,Ming Jin,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LeNEPA，一种无数据增强的时序自监督学习方法，通过潜在空间预测和SIGReg正则化替代传统对比学习中的增广和梯度停止策略。在固定食谱压力测试下，LeNEPA相比ECG调优的JEPA方法在跨域泛化上表现更优，且收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序自监督学习高度依赖视图构造和数据增强策略，这些策略编码了领域特定的不变性假设，导致方法在跨域迁移时性能退化。作者希望通过消除对增广的依赖，设计一种更通用的时序SSL范式。

Method: LeNEPA采用因果骨干网络，以无增广的潜在空间下一嵌入预测为目标函数；用SIGReg各向同性正则化替代原NEPA中的stop-gradient/EMA稳定策略；预测损失在轻量级投影空间中计算，评估时丢弃该投影头。

Result: 在固定食谱协议下，ECG调优的JEPA在PTB-XL上表现强劲但在Diag上泛化较弱，而LeNEPA在两个数据集上均保持有效增益；LeNEPA在2-5k步更新后达到最终AUROC/AUPRC增益的80%，快于JEPA的5-10k步；CauKer预训练的LeNEPA变体在UCR-128上达到77.65%准确率，接近MOMENT（77.89%）和Mantis。

Conclusion: 无增广的潜在空间预测是一种有前途的、低调参需求的时序自监督学习范式，在跨域泛化和收敛效率上优于依赖领域特定增广的方法。

Abstract: Time series are central to modern data mining applications, from industrial telemetry and server metrics to finance and physiology, yet time-series self-supervised learning often depends on view and augmentation choices that encode domain-specific invariances. We study how an SSL recipe behaves when its method-specific configuration is reused unchanged after the pretraining signal family changes, framing this as a fixed-recipe stress test rather than a comparison against optimally tuned methods. We introduce Latent Euclidean Next-Embedding Prediction Architecture (LeNEPA), a no-augmentation next-latent-token objective with a causal backbone. LeNEPA replaces the stop-gradient/EMA stabilization used by vanilla NEPA with SIGReg-based isotropy regularization and computes the predictive loss in a lightweight projected space that is discarded for evaluation. We compare LeNEPA with an ECG-tuned JEPA recipe under a fixed-horizon frozen-probe protocol on PTB-XL and Diag, a synthetic diagnostic corpus generated with Aionoscope. Both methods are retrained independently on each dataset while keeping their method-specific recipes unchanged. In this protocol, the ECG-tuned JEPA recipe is strong in-domain on PTB-XL but weaker when reused unchanged on Diag, whereas LeNEPA preserves useful frozen-probe gains on both datasets. Learning curves suggest faster early representation acquisition: LeNEPA reaches 80% of its final AUROC/AUPRC gain after 2--5k updates, compared with 5--10k updates for the faster JEPA readout. As a separate external frozen-encoder check, a CauKer-pretrained LeNEPA variant reaches 77.65% mean UCR-128 Random-Forest accuracy in a single-seed, best-checkpoint run, within 1.16 points of Mantis and within 0.24 points of MOMENT (77.89%). Overall, the results support no-augmentation latent prediction as a useful candidate recipe for low-retuning time-series SSL.

</details>


### [110] [Automatic Detection of Stress from Speech in the Trier Social Stress Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00986)
*Hanna Drimalla,Wieland R. Cremer,Christine Kraus,Oliver T. Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用Trier Social Stress Test (TSST)范式收集50名参与者的语音数据，通过说话人分离和机器学习模型，实现了对压力与非压力情境的自动区分，并部分预测了生理和情感压力反应，证明语音可以作为多维压力反应的有效非侵入性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一种非侵入性的语音压力自动检测方法，以服务于行为研究和临床评估中对压力状态的无感监测需求。

Method: 收集50名参与者（TSST压力组 vs 对照组）的语音数据，采用说话人分离（speaker diarization）和机器学习模型构建处理流水线，并通过特征重要性分析识别最具信息量的声学-韵律特征。

Result: 压力检测性能显著高于均值基线；声学-韵律特征可部分预测生理和情感压力反应。

Conclusion: 语音可作为人类压力反应多维度指标的有效、非侵入性测量手段。

Abstract: Automatically detecting stress in speech provides an unobtrusive way to gain insights relevant to behavioral research or clinical assessment. This study investigates the automatic differentiation between a stressful and non-stressful situation, and the prediction of physiological and affective stress responses. Speech data was collected from 50 participants who either completed the Trier Social Stress Test (TSST) or a non-stressful control condition. With a processing pipeline that included speaker diarization and machine learning models, we achieved stress detection performance significantly above a mean baseline. Moreover, relevant physiological and affective stress responses were partially predictable from acoustic-prosodic features. Feature-importance analyses identified the most informative predictors contributing to model performance. The findings demonstrate that speech can serve as a meaningful and unobtrusive indicator of multiple dimensions of the human stress response.

</details>


### [111] [Generative Model Proposal based Particle Filtering for Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01012)
*Chandni Nagda,Mayank Shrivastavam Gudrun Thorkelsdottir,Gan Zhang,Morteza Mardani,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Proposal Particle Filter (FPPF)，通过学习条件生成模型作为粒子滤波的建议分布，逼近方差最小化的最优建议分布，在保持贝叶斯更新和可处理似然计算的同时，有效缓解高维粒子退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粒子滤波在高维空间中容易退化（粒子塌缩），而现有生成式方法虽能学习条件状态转移但缺乏原则性的贝叶斯更新，导致长时域误差累积。需要一种既能利用生成模型表达能力又能保持正确贝叶斯后验更新的方法。

Method: FPPF学习一个条件生成模型作为建议分布，该分布近似方差最小化的最优建议分布；通过条件化观测将粒子引导至高似然区域再进行重要性加权；由于建议分布具有可处理的似然计算，可以计算精确的重要性权重并保留贝叶斯更新步骤；针对高维问题引入局部化策略。

Result: 在多种动力系统上的大量实验表明，FPPF在非线性、非高斯和高维场景下均优于统计基线和其它生成式方法。

Conclusion: FPPF通过结合生成模型的表达能力与原则性的贝叶斯更新，有效解决了传统粒子滤波在高维空间中的退化问题，为数据同化中的滤波任务提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Data assimilation models state dynamics conditioned on sequential observations, and has wide-ranging scientific applications. In the filtering setting, the goal is to model the posterior over the current state given all observations so far. Classical solutions typically make simplifying distributional or functional assumptions, e.g., linear-Gaussian systems, which can be inaccurate in many scenarios. In principle, particle filters (PFs) remove these assumptions, yet often collapse in high dimensions. Recent generative approaches learn conditional state transitions, but without principled Bayesian updates they do not recover the correct filtering posterior and can accumulate error over long horizons. In this work, we introduce Flow Proposal Particle Filters (FPPF), which learn a conditional generative model based proposal approximating the variance-minimizing optimal proposal for particle propagation. Conditioning on observations steers particles toward high-likelihood regions before weighting, reducing weight variance and delaying degeneracy. Since our proposal admits tractable likelihood evaluation, FPPF computes accurate importance weights and retains a Bayesian update step. We further extend FPPF to high-dimensional problems through localization strategies, adressing another standard PF failure mode. Extensive experiments on a variety of dynamical systems show that FPPF outperforms statistical baselines and other generative methods in non-linear, non-Gaussian, and high-dimensional regimes.

</details>


### [112] [Seahorse: A Unified Benchmarking Framework for Spatiotemporal Event Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01022)
*Yahya Aalaila,Gerrit Großmann,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEAHORSE是一个统一的神经时空点过程（STPP）可重复实验框架，通过标准化的编码-演化-解码接口和基准协议实现公平模型比较，并配套HawkesNest合成压力测试套件揭示各模型的归纳偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经STPP模型比较脆弱，各实现在前处理、坐标归一化、数据集划分、似然约定和评估协议上存在差异，缺乏标准化对比基准。

Method: 提出SEAHORSE框架，统一了编码-演化-解码（encode-evolve-decode）接口，在单一可执行基准协议下训练、调优和评估所有模型家族，并采用原始坐标似然报告；配套HawkesNest合成压力测试套件。

Result: 通过增加事件模式复杂度，成功暴露各模型家族的归纳偏差：部分模型性能严重下降，另一些保持稳定。

Conclusion: SEAHORSE实现了公平的STPP模型比较与可控的诊断研究，有助于理解不同神经STPP方法的归纳偏差特性。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal point processes (STPPs) model event data in continuous time and space, with applications in mobility, epidemiology, and public safety. Recent neural STPPs span expressive intensity models, conditional density models, continuous-time latent dynamics, normalizing-flow spatial decoders, and score-based generative mechanisms. Yet comparison remains fragile because implementations differ in preprocessing, coordinate normalization, splits, likelihood conventions, and evaluation protocols. We present SEAHORSE, a unified framework for reproducible STPP experimentation. SEAHORSE formalizes neural STPPs through a common encode-evolve-decode interface and trains, tunes, and evaluates every model family under a single executable benchmark protocol with raw-coordinate likelihood reporting. This enables fair comparisons but, more importantly, controlled diagnostic studies. We pair SEAHORSE with HawkesNest, a synthetic stress-test suite, and show that increasing event-pattern complexity exposes each family's inductive bias, degrading some models sharply and leaving others stable. Code: https://github.com/YahyaAalaila/seahorse.

</details>


### [113] [The Model Organism Lottery: Model Organism Interpretability Strongly Depends on Training Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01033)
*Andrzej Szablewski,Gabriel Konar-Steenberg,Raffaello Fornasiere,Nikita Menon,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG
