TL;DR: 本文系统研究了不同训练方法构建的模型生物（MOs）对可解释性评估的影响，发现训练目标、目标行为、模型架构等因素显著影响可解释性结果，综合训练方法得到的MOs比标准事后微调更难解释，对当前MOs作为可解释性代理的有效性提出质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明事后训练方法可能使可解释性变得不切实际地容易，现有MO构建方式可能无法有效评估可解释性方法的真实性能，因此需要系统研究不同训练方法对MO可解释性的影响。

Method: 构建了54个基于OLMo2-1B和gemma-3-1b-it的MOs，采用七种不同训练技术（包括标准事后SFT、事后DPO、以及将MO数据集成到OLMo事后训练DPO阶段等更现实的集成方法），并使用激活oracle、激活引导、logit lens和稀疏自编码器等可解释性方法进行基准测试。

Result: (i) MO可解释性强烈依赖于训练目标、目标行为、模型架构和训练数据生成流程；(ii) 即使控制目标行为表达强度的差异后，仍存在显著方差；(iii) 更现实的集成训练方法往往比标准事后方法产生更难解释的MOs。

Conclusion: 当前模型生物作为可解释性代理的有效性存在严重质疑，需要对MO构建方法进行更严谨的设计以提升可解释性评估的有效性。

Abstract: Model organisms (MOs) - language models trained to exhibit undesired or unnatural behaviours - are frequently used as testbeds for evaluating white-box interpretability techniques. Current MOs are typically constructed via post-hoc supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on behavioural transcripts or synthetic documents. Prior research has shown that interpretability methods can easily identify hidden behaviours in these MOs. However, recent work suggests that such post-hoc training methods may make interpretability unrealistically easy. We investigate this claim by constructing a suite of 54 $\verb|OLMo2-1B|$- and $\verb|gemma-3-1b-it|$-based MOs trained with seven different techniques, including standard post-hoc SFT, post-hoc DPO, and more realistic integration of MO data into the OLMo post-training DPO phase. We use these MO variants to benchmark activation oracles, activation steering, logit lens, and sparse autoencoders. Our findings show that (i) MO interpretability depends strongly on training objective, target behaviour, model architecture, and training data generation pipeline; (ii) substantial variance remains even after controlling for differences in the strength of target behaviour expression; and (iii) our more realistic $\textit{integrated training}$ often yields less interpretable MOs than standard post-hoc methods. Our results cast substantial doubt on the validity of current MOs as interpretability proxies.

</details>


### [114] [GSRQ: Gain-Shape Residual Quantization for Sub-1-bit KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01065)
*Soosung Kim,Minjae Park,Eui-Young Chung,Jaeyong Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gain-Shape K-means (GSKM)替代标准K-means，解决向量量化中质心收缩导致的角对齐问题，并构建Gain-Shape Residual Quantization (GSRQ)用于KV缓存压缩。在LLaMA-3-8B上，GSRQ在1-bit下将LongBench平均准确率从VQLLM的11.34提升至33.54。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差向量量化(RQ)方法依赖标准ℓ2 K-means作为码本学习基元，但该基元在高维空间中存在质心收缩问题，削弱了ℓ2失真中的角对齐项，导致方向保持困难，从而影响KV缓存压缩质量。

Method: 1) 提出Gain-Shape K-means (GSKM)，作为K-means的即插即用替代，通过解耦增益(gain)和形状(shape)改进方向保真度；2) 将GSKM的加权扩展纳入残差量化(RQ)流水线，构建Gain-Shape Residual Quantization (GSRQ)。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B上，GSRQ在多种比特率下显著超越现有KV缓存量化基线。在1-bit设定下，LongBench任务平均准确率从VQLLM的11.34提升至33.54，增益达22.20个百分点。

Conclusion: GSKM有效解决了标准K-means在高维量化中的质心收缩问题，GSRQ通过改进方向保真度实现了KV缓存的极低位压缩，在1-bit存储率下仍保持较高任务性能。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) with extended context windows is increasingly constrained by the linear growth of Key-Value (KV) cache memory. Vector Quantization (VQ), particularly Residual Quantization (RQ), is a promising approach for pushing KV cache storage toward the sub-1-bit regime by progressively encoding residuals with small codebooks. However, most VQ methods still rely on standard $\ell_2$ $K$-means as the core codebook-learning primitive. We identify a subtle high-dimensional issue of this primitive: Euclidean centroid averaging can induce centroid shrinkage, which weakens the angular alignment term in the $\ell_2$ distortion and makes directional preservation harder. To address this issue, we propose Gain-Shape $K$-means (GSKM), a drop-in replacement for $K$-means that improves directional fidelity while matching, and in some regimes improving, $\ell_2$ distortion. We then build Gain-Shape Residual Quantization (GSRQ) by incorporating a weighted extension of GSKM into an RQ pipeline. On LLaMA-3-8B, GSRQ substantially improves over strong KV cache quantization baselines across bit rates. At 1-bit, it improves the average accuracy across LongBench tasks from 11.34 to 33.54, a gain of 22.20 percentage points over VQLLM.

</details>


### [115] [Balancing Expressivity and Learnability in Quantum Kernel Bandit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01080)
*Yuqi Huang,Vincent Y. F. Tan,Sharu Theresa Jose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于量子核的高斯过程（GP） bandit 优化问题，针对NISQ时代任务中全量量子核复杂度高、累积遗憾大的问题，提出投影量子核与经典核近似方法，在降低特征维度的同时保留量子特性，并推导了误设定GP bandit算法的遗憾界，实现了样本效率与计算开销的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ-era任务（如量子控制、量子态制备、变分量子算法）中，量子核虽可通过领域特定的归纳偏置提供“量子优势”，但直接使用全量高维量子核会显著增加模型复杂度和信息增益，导致累积遗憾高、可学习性差，亟需有效的降维与近似方法。

Method: 提出投影量子核与经典核近似技术，在降低特征维度的同时保留关键量子特性；基于近似核开发误设定GP bandit算法，并推导刻画近似误差与信息增益之间权衡的遗憾界。

Result: 所提方法在样本效率上优于全量量子核，同时大幅降低计算开销，并提供了选择最优模型复杂度的原则性指导。

Conclusion: 通过投影量子核与经典核近似，可在保持量子优势的前提下显著提升GP bandit优化的可扩展性，为量子原生应用提供了高效实用的优化方案。

Abstract: We investigate Gaussian process (GP) bandit optimization with quantum kernels, assuming the mean reward function lies in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) induced by the quantum kernel. This setting is motivated by NISQ-era tasks such as quantum control, state preparation and variational quantum algorithms. While quantum kernels can offer a `quantum advantage' via domain-specific inductive biases, naïvely using full, high-dimensional kernels increases model complexity and information gain, leading to higher cumulative regret and poor learnability. To address this, we propose projected quantum kernels and classical kernel approximation techniques that reduce feature dimensionality while preserving key quantum properties. Using these approximate kernels, we develop misspecified GP bandit algorithms and derive regret bounds that characterize the trade-off between approximation error and information gain. The regret bounds provide principled guidance for selecting the optimal model complexity. Empirically, our methods outperform full quantum kernels in sample efficiency, while substantially reducing computational overhead, enabling scalable GP optimization for quantum-native applications.

</details>


### [116] [When Context Compensates for Sparse Event History: AlphaEarth for Spatio-Temporal Point-Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01082)
*Yahya Aalaila,Mouad Elhamdi,Gerrit Großmann,Daniel Jenson,Elizaveta Semenova,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究外生空间上下文（AlphaEarth嵌入）能否在局部事件历史稀疏时补偿时空点过程模型的泛化能力。在EMS预测任务中，AE嵌入在跨区域迁移时显著提升性能，尤其在历史窗口短（1-2周）时提升2-6倍，长窗口（20-104周）时仍有10-20%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空点过程模型在局部事件历史稀疏时泛化困难，需要研究外生空间上下文信息能否补偿这一不足。

Method: 以固定对数高斯Cox过程为骨干，对比纯事件模型与添加AlphaEarth嵌入作为线性空间上下文的增强模型，在EMS数据上对8个保留区域进行空间迁移评估，通过变化历史窗口长度w进行实验。

Result: AE嵌入在所有历史窗口下均提升跨区域预测性能，在历史稀疏（1-2周）时提升约2-6倍，在历史充足（20-104周）时提升约10-20%。

Conclusion: 外生上下文信息能在事件历史有限时显著稳定空间迁移的点过程预测。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal point-process models must often generalise across space when local event histories are sparse. We study whether exogenous spatial context can compensate in such regimes. Using a fixed log-Gaussian Cox process backbone, we compare an event-only model with the same model augmented by AlphaEarth embeddings as linear spatial context. We evaluate spatial transfer on emergency medical services (EMS) forecasting across eight held-out regions, fixed forecast anchors, and a sweep over history length $w$, using only AlphaEarth (AE) embeddings available strictly before each anchor. AE improves out-of-region predictive performance across all history regimes, with the largest gains under scarce histories: approximately $2$--$6\times$ multiplicative improvements at $1-2$ weeks, tapering to roughly $10$--$20\%$ at $w=20$--$104$ weeks. These results show that contextual information can substantially stabilise spatially transferred point-process forecasts when event history is limited.

</details>


### [117] [SynLaD: Latent Diffusion for Generating Synthesizable Molecules Conditioned on 3D Pharmacophore Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01105)
*Miruna Cretu,John Bradshaw,Patricia Suriana,Saeed Saremi,Omar Mahmood,Kirill Shmilovich,Kangway Chuang,Vishnu Sresht,Colin Grambow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SynLaD是一个将配体药物设计目标（要制造什么）与合成可及性（如何制造）统一起来的潜在扩散框架，通过反应约束生成和药效团条件3D设计，在生物活性配体的类似物生成任务中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小分子生成模型通常优化药物设计目标或合成可及性中的单一指标，导致难以同时发现高评分且可合成的分子，造成瓶颈。

Method: SynLaD构建了一个编码器将分子映射到潜在空间，使用双解码器头：几何头重建原子类型和坐标，自回归合成头输出基于反应符号的合成路线；扩散Transformer在药效团条件下生成新潜在表示。

Result: 在生物活性配体的类似物生成任务中，SynLaD在可合成性和多样性命中生成方面优于现有基线，能够产生形状对齐且具有可行合成计划的分子。

Conclusion: 单一模型可以同时实现配体形状对齐和可行合成路径规划，解决了药物设计中"制造什么"与"如何制造"的脱节问题。

Abstract: We present SynLaD, a latent diffusion framework for small-molecule generation that unifies ligand-based drug design objectives (what to make) with synthetic accessibility (how to make it). Current models typically optimize one objective at the expense of the other, creating a bottleneck for discovering high-scoring and synthesizable molecules. SynLaD combines reaction-constrained generation with pharmacophore-conditioned 3D design by learning a latent space that decodes to both 3D structures and synthesis pathways. An encoder maps molecules to a latent representation used by two decoder heads: (i) a geometric head that reconstructs atom types and coordinates and (ii) an autoregressive synthesis head that outputs synthetic routes in a serialized, reaction-based notation. A diffusion transformer generates novel latents in the learned space, conditioned on pharmacophore profiles. Across analogue generation tasks for bioactive ligands, SynLaD outperforms existing baselines in synthesizable and diverse hit generation, demonstrating that a single model can produce shape-aligned molecules with feasible synthesis plans.

</details>


### [118] [GAIA: Geometry-Adaptive Operator Learning for Forward and Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01128)
*Meenakshi Krishnan,Pranav Pulijala,Ke Chen,Haizhao Yang,Ramani Duraiswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAIA模型，一种通过几何令牌和交叉注意力机制实现几何自适应积分算子的方法，能够统一处理任意几何上的正向（含边界值问题）和逆向问题，在七个基准测试中取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何自适应神经算子主要针对输入输出共享同一空间域的正向问题设计，无法有效处理边界值问题和逆向问题（输入输出可能位于不同域）。

Method: 将域边界和内部场分布编码为几何令牌，通过交叉注意力机制让积分变换层根据这些令牌进行局部自适应核调整，构建单一架构统一处理正向和逆向问题。

Result: 在七个2D/3D基准测试中，在逆向和BVP任务上均取得SOTA：机翼流重建中位相对L2误差降低64%，EIT降低27%，在所有MCB形状类别上优于所有基线。

Conclusion: GAIA能够单次前向推理处理任意域上的正向和逆向问题，无需重训练、迭代优化或图构建，在逆向和BVP任务上显著超越现有方法，在前向任务上与专用求解器竞争力相当。

Abstract: Operator learning for partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary geometries builds fast neural surrogates for large-scale simulation. Although recent geometry-adaptive neural operators have made substantial progress, they are mainly designed for forward problems in which inputs and outputs share the same spatial domain. This limits their applicability for boundary value problems (BVPs) and inverse problems, where inputs and outputs may live on different domains. We introduce the Geometry-Adaptive Integral Autoencoder (GAIA), an operator learning model that encodes the domain boundary and the interior field distribution into geometry tokens, and conditions integral transform layers on these tokens via cross-attention, allowing the kernel to adapt locally to geometric features. This yields a single architecture for forward (including BVPs) and inverse problems on arbitrary domains in one pass, without retraining, iterative optimization, or graph construction. We evaluate GAIA on seven 2D and 3D benchmarks, four of which are new or substantially extended benchmarks for inverse problems and BVP: electrical impedance tomography, optical tomography, 3D Darcy flow on varying geometries, and a modified setting of Poisson BVP on mechanical components benchmark (MCB). GAIA sets new state-of-the-art results on every inverse and BVP task, reducing median relative $L^2$ error by 64% on airfoil flow reconstruction and 27% on EIT relative to the next best amortized method, and outperforming all baselines on every shape category of MCB. On other forward problems, GAIA is competitive with specialized solvers while maintaining stable accuracy across point resolutions on which transformer-based baselines degrade.

</details>


### [119] [A Lightweight Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Multivariate Time Series using Hierarchical-JEPA on ECG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01145)
*Siwon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ER-JEPA，一种轻量级自监督学习框架，用于多变量时间序列（特别是12导联心电图）分析。该框架采用两级层次结构，灵感来源于心脏病专家的诊断方法，通过两个联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的级联实现多级抽象表示。在约18万条10秒心电记录上预训练后，在ST-MEM基准上达到SOTA性能，且计算速度快、资源消耗低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域常面临目标数据集有限而大量未标注数据分布泛化的情况，自监督学习能有效利用大规模数据。作者希望通过模拟心脏病专家的诊断逻辑（先分析单个心搏事件，再考察全局序列），设计一种轻量级且高效的SSL框架用于心电图分析。

Method: ER-JEPA采用两级层次结构：第一阶段为每个时间区间构建表示，第二阶段将这些表示作为单变量时间序列处理。核心包含两个级联的JEPA（构成H-JEPA），使用Vision Transformer（ViT）作为骨干网络。预训练采用约18万条10秒12导联心电记录。

Result: 在ST-MEM基准测试上取得了最先进的（state-of-the-art）下游任务性能，同时实现了快速计算和极低资源消耗。

Conclusion: ER-JEPA（H-JEPA）能有效编码多级抽象表示，在ECG多变量时间序列分析任务上表现优异，且计算效率高，适合资源受限场景。

Abstract: Data analysis in the medical domain often encounters scenarios involving a limited target dataset and a large, unannotated dataset with a general distribution. Under such circumstances, self-supervised learning (SSL) methods are highly effective for utilizing large datasets, making them a popular choice for electrocardiogram (ECG) analysis. This work presents the Event Reconstruction Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (ER-JEPA), a lightweight SSL framework for multivariate time series, whose name and two-fold hierarchical structure are inspired by the diagnostic approach of cardiologists. At its core, ER-JEPA features: (1) a two-stage structure that constructs representations for each time interval and subsequently processes these representations as a univariate time series, (2) the hierarchical integration of two Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), and (3) a Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone. The structural concatenation of two JEPAs categorizes the model as a Hierarchical JEPA (H-JEPA), designed to encode multiple levels of abstract representations for enhanced prediction on complex tasks. This study reports a successful application of H-JEPA to 12-lead ECG data as a multivariate time series alongside an analysis of the sensitivity of hierarchical representation during the pretraining stage. Pretrained on approximately 180,000 10-second recordings, the model achieves state-of-the-art downstream performance on the ST-MEM benchmark, with rapid computation and minimal resource usage.

</details>


### [120] [Efficient Compression of Structured and Unstructured Volumes via Learned 3D Gaussian Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01164)
*Landon Dyken,Sharmistha Chakrabarti,Nathan Debardeleben,Steve Petruzza,Qi Wu,Will Usher,Sidharth Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D高斯原语的显式体积数据压缩模型，通过将3D高斯集合重新解释为标量场的显式表示，结合加权聚合采样策略和CUDA加速管线，实现了无需网格存储的高效压缩，在非结构化体积数据上全面超越现有隐式神经表示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于隐式神经表示(INR)的非结构化体积压缩方法不编码几何信息，需要部分网格存储用于后续采样，限制了压缩率；而新视角合成方法表明3D高斯的显式集合可用于体积数据可视化。作者希望将3D高斯应用于体积数据压缩，以消除网格存储需求并提升压缩性能。

Method: 1) 将3D高斯集合重新解释为标量场的显式表示；2) 通过空间位置处相交高斯函数的加权聚合重建标量值；3) 开发优化的CUDA加速管线用于结构化和非结构化模型采样；4) 设计鼓励精确域编码的损失函数；5) 提出基于采样误差的增密策略。

Result: 在结构化体积数据上，所提显式模型在重建质量上与现有INR相当，但训练速度显著提升；在非结构化体积数据上，所有评估指标均明显优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 显式3D高斯模型天然编码了域几何信息，消除了非结构化体积的网格存储需求，带来了更高的压缩机会，是一种有效的体积数据压缩新范式。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that implicit neural representations (INRs) can be trained to effectively compress structured and unstructured volume data, allowing for direct data querying with a reduced memory footprint. However, as existing INRs for unstructured volumes do not encode geometry, they require partial mesh storage for later sampling, limiting achievable compression. At the same time, novel view synthesis methods have shown that explicit collections of 3D Gaussians can be used to accurately visualize volume data. In this work, we introduce an explicit model for volume data compression based on 3D Gaussian primitives. We reinterpret collections of 3D Gaussians as an explicit representation of a scalar field and use a sampling strategy that reconstructs scalar values at spatial locations through weighted aggregation of intersecting Gaussians. We develop optimized CUDA-accelerated pipelines for structured and unstructured model sampling, loss functions that encourage accurate domain encoding by our models, and a novel sampling-error based densification strategy. Our explicit formulation naturally encodes domain geometry, eliminating the need for mesh storage in unstructured volumes and introducing significantly higher compression opportunities. Compared to existing INRs, we demonstrate that our explicit model achieves competitive reconstruction quality with significant training speedups on structured volumes, while markedly outperforming in all metrics on unstructured volumes.

</details>


### [121] [QuasiMoTTo: Quasi-Monte Carlo Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01179)
*Michael Y. Li,Anthony Zhan,Kanishk Gandhi,Noah D. Goodman,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QuasiMoTTo，通过使用拟蒙特卡洛(QMC)方法生成相关性但边际分布正确的样本，替代传统的独立同分布(i.i.d.)采样，在推理计算和强化学习中显著提升样本效率。在四个推理基准上，QuasiMoTTo以25-47%更少的样本达到与i.i.d.相同的pass@k准确率，在策略梯度RL中减少50%训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展推理计算时，生成大量独立并行尝试虽然有效但成本高昂，因为独立采样会导致冗余解浪费计算资源。这种看似不可避免的权衡实际上并非根本性限制，存在可以生成相关但精确样本的并行采样器设计空间，本文旨在探索这一空间以提高样本效率。

Method: 将自回归采样重新参数化为逆CDF采样，用拟蒙特卡洛(QMC)方法生成底层均匀随机数，使样本在输出空间中分布更均匀、减少冗余。样本间虽存在相关性，但每个样本边际分布仍与语言模型一致，因此可直接用于策略梯度训练。同时开发了无偏bootstrap估计器来评估相关采样器的pass@k。

Result: 在四个推理基准上，QuasiMoTTo以25-47%更少的样本达到i.i.d.的pass@k准确率，且常达到边际保持采样器的pass@k理论上界。在GRPO策略梯度强化学习中，以50%更少训练步数匹配i.i.d.性能。

Conclusion: 相关采样是提升推理计算和强化学习中样本效率的有效途径，更高的样本覆盖率带来更强的每批学习信号，且QuasiMoTTo可作为i.i.d.采样的直接替代方案。

Abstract: Scaling inference compute, by generating many parallel attempts per problem, is a costly but reliable lever for improving language model capabilities. By default these attempts are generated independently, wasting inference compute on redundant solutions. This waste seems unavoidable. After all, independence is what makes parallel sampling trivial to scale. However, this tradeoff is not fundamental: there is a rich design space of samplers that generate correlated but exact samples entirely in parallel. We explore this design space as an avenue for improving sample efficiency in scaling inference compute and reinforcement learning (RL). Concretely, we introduce QuasiMoTTo, which uses correlated samples as a drop-in replacement for i.i.d. samples. To generate these samples, QuasiMoTTo uses a reparameterization of autoregressive sampling as inverse-CDF sampling and draws the underlying uniforms with quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC); because QMC spreads the uniforms out more evenly than i.i.d., the resulting samples cover the output space with far less redundancy. Even though the batch is correlated, each sample is marginally distributed according to the language model, so we can use the batch for policy-gradient training. Our empirical analysis focuses on understanding how efficiently QuasiMoTTo can turn compute into performance. To evaluate correlated samplers, whose dependence breaks standard pass@k estimators, we first develop an unbiased bootstrap estimator. Across four reasoning benchmarks, QuasiMoTTo matches i.i.d. pass@k accuracy with 25-47% fewer samples. Strikingly, QuasiMoTTo often saturates an upper bound on pass@k that holds for any marginal-preserving sampler. We also apply QuasiMoTTo to policy-gradient RL (GRPO) where it matches i.i.d. performance with 50% fewer training steps. These gains come from higher coverage, which yields a stronger learning signal per batch.

</details>


### [122] [Neural Certificate Pricing for Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01185)
*Jingyi Chen,Xinyuan Zhang,Xinwu Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经证书定价（NCP）框架，通过无监督学习利用组合优化中“解结构可验证但难搜索”的不对称性，将神经网络训练的证书级对偶价格经结构化恢复层重构出可行原始边际，实现摊销分离。在三类CO问题上，NCP大幅超越或匹敌现有神经方法，且泛化性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题的难点在于离散结构导致指数级搜索空间，但解的结构可行性可在多项式时间内验证。这种不对称性未被现有方法充分利用，且传统神经方法在处理可行性和泛化性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出NCP框架：1）神经网络预测证书级对偶价格；2）结构化恢复层（基于分离的摊销）从对偶价格重构原始边际解；3）无监督学习，利用证书一致性条件保证恢复的解全局可行；4）理论证明预测价格的一阶误差仅导致目标值的二阶损失。

Result: 在三类组合优化问题（路径、打包、覆盖）上，NCP或大幅超越现有最先进神经基线，或以极低计算时间达到相当性能，且展现出更强的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: NCP通过将分离过程摊销为学习证书级残差价格，有效利用CO中验证易搜索难的不对称性，为神经求解器提供了兼具理论保证、高效计算和强泛化性的新范式。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization (CO) problems are difficult because certifiable discrete structure induces exponential search. One needs to search over the set exponentially many candidates to certify optimality, however, the structural feasibility of a path, packing, or cover can be verified in polynomial time once supplied. In this study, we introduce Neural Certificate Pricing (NCP) that exploits this asymmetry under an unsupervised learning framework. A neural network is trained to predict certificate-level dual prices, while a structured recovery layer constructs the induced primal marginal. NCP can be viewed as amortized separation: instead of enumerating violated inequalities, it learns the residual prices through which their aggregate effect enters recovery. When the certificate-consistency condition holds, the recovered marginal is globally feasible, and a local theory shows that first-order errors in the predicted price induce only second-order loss in objective value. Across three classes of CO problems, NCP either outperforms state-of-the-art neural baselines by large margins or matches them at a fraction of the computation time, and shows stronger out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [123] [Quantum vs. Classical Machine Learning: A Unified Empirical Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01197)
*Chuanming Yu,Jiaming Liu,Zihao Ge,Xiongfei Wu,Lulu Zhu,Pengzhan Zhao,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过七组监督学习和强化学习模型的对比实验，实证研究了量子机器学习（QML）模型与经典模型的性能差异。结果表明，QML模型在预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间上尚未超越经典基线，但在噪声过滤和假阳性控制方面展现出潜力。研究总结了QML在硬件环境、训练效率和收敛稳定性方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于QML模型相对于经典模型性能优势的证据不足，亟需系统性的实证研究来评估QML的实际表现。

Method: 选取七组模型对（覆盖监督学习和强化学习），将QML模型与其对应的经典基线模型进行对比实验，评估预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间等指标。

Result: QML模型在整体预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间上未超越经典基线；但在噪声过滤和假阳性控制方面表现更好。

Conclusion: 当前QML尚未在性能上超越经典模型，但在特定任务（如噪声过滤）上有潜力；研究揭示了QML在硬件、训练效率和收敛稳定性方面的挑战，为后续鲁棒性和参数优化研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum computing has emerged as a promising computational paradigm for machine learning (ML), with the potential to offer computational advantages over classical approaches. At this stage, the evidence supporting the performance and advantages of quantum machine learning (QML) models relative to classical models is insufficient.To address this gap, this paper presents an empirical study on the performance of QML models and their classical counterparts. We compare seven model pairs spanning supervised learning and reinforcement learning. Our results indicate that the evaluated quantum machine learning models do not yet surpass the classical baselines in overall prediction performance, policy stability, or training time. Nevertheless, QML remains a promising approach for filtering noise and controlling false positives. Our research findings summarize the challenges facing quantum machine learning across hardware environments, training efficiency, and convergence stability, providing a foundation for research into the robustness and parameter optimization of QML. This work is publicly available at https://github.com/Z-537-437/QML.

</details>


### [124] [TiRex-2: Generalizing TiRex to Multivariate Data and Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01204)
*Patrick Podest,Marco Pichler,Elias Bürger,Levente Zólyomi,Bernhard Voggenberger,Wilhelm Berghammer,Daniel Klotz,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TiRex-2 是一种基于 xLSTM 的循环时间序列基础模型，支持多变量预测和协变量整合，具有恒定每块计算成本的流式处理能力，在零样本任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Transformer 的时间序列基础模型在捕获跨变量依赖时具有二次复杂度和全历史重计算开销，难以高效应对实时流式预测场景。

Method: 采用记忆为中心的循环设计（xLSTM），结合双向时间混合器与非对称分组注意力变量混合器，实现未来协变量整合与严格因果性约束；提出合成耦合管道从单变量语料库在线生成多变量样本以支持可扩展预训练。

Result: 在 GIFT-Eval 和 fev-bench 上达到零样本 SOTA；流式推理时上下文长度可任意扩展且推理成本保持恒定；单变量模式激活 38.4M 参数，多变量模式额外激活 44.1M 参数。

Conclusion: TiRex-2 是首个同时具备流式高效性、多变量协变量整合与严格因果性的时间序列基础模型，为实时预测提供了一种新的计算高效范式。

Abstract: We introduce TiRex-2, a recurrent xLSTM-based time series foundation model that generalizes the univariate TiRex to multivariate forecasting with both past and future covariates. Real-world forecasting is inherently sequential: observations arrive continuously, variables evolve jointly, and a subset of covariates is known ahead of time. Existing Transformer-based time series foundation models capture cross-variate dependencies but incur quadratic complexity in context length and require full-history recomputation as new observations arrive. TiRex-2 addresses these limitations through a memory-centric recurrent design that operates at constant per-patch cost under streaming. The model combines a bidirectional time mixer with an asymmetric grouped-attention variate mixer, enabling the integration of future-known covariates while preserving strict causality over target variables. To our knowledge, this is the first time series foundation model that achieves this combination of properties. To support scalable multivariate pretraining, we propose a synthetic coupling pipeline that composes diverse multivariate samples on the fly from large univariate corpora. Empirically, TiRex-2 achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench, remains stable when streamed to arbitrary context lengths, and maintains constant inference cost per patch. The model uses 38.4M active parameters in univariate mode, with an additional 44.1M parameters activated for multivariate forecasting.

</details>


### [125] [Is One Layer Enough? Training A Single Transformer Layer Can Match Full-Parameter RL Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01232)
*Zijian Zhang,Rizhen Hu,Athanasios Glentis,Dawei Li,Chung-Yiu Yau,Hongzhou Lin,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM的RL后训练中，单层transformer训练即可恢复甚至超越全参数训练的大部分收益，且高贡献层集中在模型中部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法通常均匀更新所有参数，隐含假设各层对RL训练收益贡献相似，本文旨在挑战这一假设，探究RL适应在各transformer层中的分布情况。

Method: 提出"层贡献"（layer contribution）量化指标，测量单层独立训练所恢复的全参数RL训练收益比例；在Qwen3/Qwen2.5两个模型家族、GRPO/GiGPO/Dr. GRPO三种RL算法、数学推理/代码生成/智能体决策等多任务领域上进行系统性逐层实验。

Result: RL训练收益高度集中在少数（甚至单个）transformer层；高贡献层集中在transformer堆栈中部，靠近输入输出端的层贡献显著较低；层排名在数据集、任务、模型家族和RL算法间高度相关。

Conclusion: 挑战了各层均匀贡献的默认假设，揭示了RL后训练收益集中在模型中间层的稳定结构模式，为高效RL微调提供了新视角。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central component of post-training large language models (LLMs), yet little is understood about how RL adaptation is distributed across transformer layers. Existing approaches typically update all model parameters uniformly, implicitly assuming that every layer contributes similarly to the gains obtained during RL post-training. In this work, we challenge this assumption through a systematic layer-wise study of RL training. Surprisingly, we find that training a single transformer layer can recover most of the gains achieved by full-parameter RL training, and in some cases even surpass it. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the quantity layer contribution, which measures the fraction of full RL improvement recovered by training a layer in isolation. Across seven models spanning two model families (Qwen3, Qwen2.5), three RL algorithms (GRPO, GiGPO, Dr. GRPO), and multiple task domains including mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic decision-making, we observe a remarkably stable pattern: RL gains are highly concentrated in a small subset of, and in many cases even a single, transformer layers. More strikingly, the same structural pattern consistently emerges: high-contribution layers concentrate in the middle of the transformer stack, while layers near the input and output ends contribute substantially less. The resulting layer rankings remain strongly correlated across datasets, tasks, model families, and RL algorithms.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [A Priori Integral Persistent Excitation in Conservative Polynomial ODEs with Higher-Order Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00091)
*Aleksandr Semenov,Alexander Fradkov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对保守多项式常微分方程的先验积分持续激励验证方法，无需参数先验知识。提供了严格的定理证明，并通过高阶交互非线性动力学系统实例分析了该方法的应用。主要结果的证明已在Lean形式化验证语言中完成形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 积分持续激励在非线性动力系统的参数辨识与自适应控制中至关重要，但现有方法通常需要参数先验知识或无法处理保守多项式ODE系统。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一种无需参数先验知识的验证方法。

Method: 针对保守多项式常微分方程系统，提出了一种先验的积分持续激励验证方法。方法不依赖系统参数的先验知识。通过严格的数学定理证明建立理论框架，并在Lean形式化验证语言中对主要定理的证明进行了形式化编码，确保证明的严谨性和可验证性。

Result: 成功提出了适用于保守多项式ODE的积分持续激励验证方法；给出了严格的定理证明；通过一个具有高阶交互的非线性动力学系统实例展示了方法的有效性；主要定理的证明已在Lean中完成形式化验证。

Conclusion: 该方法为保守多项式ODE系统的积分持续激励验证提供了一种无需参数先验知识的有效途径，形式化验证进一步增强了理论结果的可靠性，对参数辨识和自适应控制领域具有重要价值。

Abstract: The paper proposes an approach for verifying integral persistent excitation, which is important in problems of parameter identification and adaptive control in nonlinear dynamical systems. The approach works for conservative polynomial ODEs a priori without knowledge of the parameters. Rigorous proofs of the corresponding theorems are provided. An example of a nonlinear dynamical system with higher-order interactions and the application of the proposed method to it are analyzed. The proof of the main result is formalized in the Lean formal verification language.

</details>


### [127] [Homogenization of $\ell_2$-Adversarial Training in High-Dimensions: Exact Dynamics under Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00207)
*Fabrizzio Sabelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为ℓ₂对抗训练中单指标模型在高维极限下的SGD学习动态建立了分析框架，推导了SGD迭代统计量（包括对抗风险、距离最优性等）的确定性等价，通过ODE系统描述其演化，并研究了Polyak步长和精确线搜索两种学习率调度。在ℓ₂对抗最小二乘问题中，发现无常数学习率能保证单调下降，识别了各向异性协方差和岭参数不匹配是精确线搜索次优性的来源，并引入了对抗同质化SDE来描述迭代演化，最终证明收敛时极限对抗风险和SGD迭代由不动点方程决定，等价于一个自适应正则化的岭回归问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ℓ₂对抗训练在高维设置下的学习动态，特别是SGD的收敛行为、最优学习率调度以及对抗风险与标准风险之间的差异。

Method: 从统计力学视角出发，利用确定性等价方法将SGD迭代的高维随机动态映射为低维ODE系统；引入对抗同质化SDE捕捉迭代统计量的演化；分析Polyak步长和精确线搜索两种理想化学习率调度。

Result: 1) 无噪声标准最小二乘中，无常数学习率能保证SGD单调下降至对抗风险最小化器；2) 各向异性协方差和岭参数不匹配是精确线搜索相比Polyak步长次优的主要原因；3) 对抗同质化SDE表明对抗风险的演化等价于带有自适应学习率和自适应ℓ₂正则化的标准最小二乘SGD；4) 收敛时极限对抗风险由不动点方程确定，极限迭代等价于岭回归解，正则化参数为SGD的极限有效正则化。

Conclusion: 建立了ℓ₂对抗训练SGD动态的完整分析框架，揭示了对抗训练与标准训练在动态行为和收敛特性上的本质区别，为理解对抗训练的正则化效应和学习率设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We develop a framework for analyzing the learning dynamics of $\ell_2$-adversarial training of single-index models on Gaussian mixtures in the high-dimensional limit under streaming stochastic gradient descent (SGD). We derive deterministic equivalents for a broad class of statistics of the SGD iterates, including the adversarial risk and distance to adversarial optimality, in terms of the solution to a system of ODEs. We use them to study two idealized learning rate schedules: the Polyak stepsize and exact line search. In the case of $\ell_2$-adversarial least squares with a single class, we show that, unlike noiseless standard least squares, no constant learning rate guarantees monotone descent of SGD towards a minimizer of the adversarial risk. We identify anisotropic covariance and a mismatch in ridge parameters as the main sources of suboptimality of exact line search relative to the Polyak stepsize. We also introduce a stochastic differential equation (SDE), called adversarial homogenized SGD, that captures the evolution of statistics of the iterates of SGD. For $\ell_2$-adversarial least squares, using this SDE, we show the evolution of the risk is equivalent, up to dimension-free constants, to that of SGD on standard least squares with an adaptive learning rate and adaptive $\ell_2$-regularization. When the dynamics converge, the limiting adversarial risk and SGD iterate are determined by a fixed-point equation, with the limiting iterate being equivalent to the solution of a ridge regression problem whose regularization parameter is the limiting effective regularization of SGD.

</details>


### [128] [Killing the Case for Randomization in Dynamic Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00328)
*Mikhail Fadin,Huseyin Topaloglu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去随机化算法，能将任何基于采样的、不考虑库存的动态品类优化策略转化为确定性策略而不损失性能，并进一步给出可在更大范围内搜索确定性序列的变体。作为核心理论贡献，证明了任何局部最优的确定性策略相对于最优采样策略具有1/2 - epsilon的性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态品类优化问题中，基于采样的库存无关策略虽然有效，但采样过程引入了额外的性能不确定性。为解决这一问题，作者希望去除采样带来的随机性，同时不损失策略性能。

Method: 1) 提出一种去随机化算法，将任意基于采样的库存无关策略转化为确定性的品类序列，性能不退化。2) 给出该算法的变体，可在原策略支撑集之外搜索确定性品类序列，且只要能够求解静态品类优化问题即可高效实现。3) 研究局部最优确定性策略，即改变其中任何一个品类都不能提高期望总收益的策略，并证明其性能保证。

Result: 任何局部最优的确定性策略相对于最优采样策略具有1/2 - epsilon的性能保证。

Conclusion: 去随机化算法可以消除采样带来的不确定性，且局部最优的确定性策略具有接近最优采样策略一半性能的理论保证，为动态品类优化提供了一种确定性的高效替代方案。

Abstract: One of the traditional approaches for constructing approximate policies for dynamic assortment optimization problems is to use sampling-based inventory-agnostic policies. Such policies are called sampling-based, as they sample an assortment of products from a fixed distribution at each time period to offer to a customer of each type. Such policies are called inventory-agnostic, as the sampled assortments may include products without remaining inventories, so if a customer chooses a product without remaining inventories, then she leaves without a purchase. Inventory-agnostic nature of a policy is not a concern, because it is known that if the policy samples an assortment that includes products without remaining inventories, then dropping the products without remaining inventories does not degrade the performance. However, sampling-based nature of a policy is a concern, because sampling brings another source of uncertainty in the performance. In this paper, we give an algorithm to de-randomize any sampling-based inventory-agnostic policy, so the de-randomized policy offers a deterministic sequence of assortments within the support of the original policy without degrading the performance. Furthermore, we give a variation of our de-randomization algorithm that searches for a deterministic sequence of assortments beyond the support of the original policy. We show that we can implement the latter variation efficiently as long as we can solve the static assortment optimization problem under the choice model governing the choice process of the customers. As our crowning technical contribution, we study locally-optimal deterministic policies, where changing any single one of the assortments in the policy does not improve the total expected revenue. We show that any locally-optimal policy has a performance guarantee of 1/2 - epsilon when compared with the best sampling-based policy.

</details>


### [129] [Regularized Model Predictive Control via Contractivity and Implicit Lur'e Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00383)
*Ryotaro Shima,Anand Gokhale,Alexander Davydov,Francesco Bullo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对正则化模型预测控制（MPC）提出了一种基于收缩理论的稳定性分析方法，通过将正则化MPC解释为隐式Lur'e系统，推导了线性矩阵不等式条件，为三类正则化器提供了形式化的稳定性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化MPC的反馈律由有限时域最优控制问题定义，但正则化项会扰动最优性条件，导致稳定性分析困难。现有方法缺乏统一的收缩理论框架来保证此类系统的稳定性。

Method: 将正则化MPC重新解释为隐式Lur'e系统，其中正则化成本扰动最优性条件；开发了基于乘子的隐式Lur'e系统收缩框架；推导了适用于三类正则化器（凸光滑阶段成本、凸闭真阶段成本、Lipschitz梯度可微正则化器）的LMI条件。

Result: 在输入和状态软惩罚、硬输入约束、稀疏性促进惩罚等数值研究中，正则化在保持形式化收缩稳定性保证的同时，有效塑造了闭环性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于收缩理论的框架为正则化MPC提供了统一的稳定性分析方法，能够处理多种正则化器，并在保持稳定性保证的同时提升闭环性能。

Abstract: This paper develops a contraction-based stability analysis for regularized model predictive control (MPC), whose feedback law is defined implicitly by a finite-horizon optimal control problem with an additional regularizing cost. The proposed approach interprets regularized MPC as an implicit Lur'e system, in which the regularizing cost perturbs the optimality conditions. We develop a multiplier-based contraction framework for implicit Lur'e systems and derive linear matrix inequality conditions for regularized MPC with three broad classes of regularizers: convex smooth stage costs, convex closed proper stage costs, and differentiable regularizers with Lipschitz gradients. Numerical studies on input and state soft penalties, hard input constraints, and sparsity-promoting penalties illustrate that regularization shapes closed-loop performance while retaining formal contraction-based stability guarantees.

</details>


### [130] [L2-L2-gain bounds for quadratic output systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00552)
*Birgit Hillebrecht*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文推导了线性时不变系统（输出为状态和输入的二次函数）的L2-L2增益的显式界。对于纯二次输出，该界等于双变量传递函数沿频域反对角线(s, -s)的L2范数，并展示了如何通过求解线性矩阵方程来计算该界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这类系统广泛存在于端口哈密顿系统、最优控制和随机问题中，需要一种实用的工具来评估和简化二次输出模型。

Method: 通过推导线性时不变系统（输出为状态和输入的二次函数）的显式界，将纯二次输出情况下的界等价为双变量传递函数沿频域反对角线的L2范数，并通过求解线性矩阵方程实现计算。

Result: 得到了二次输出系统L2-L2增益的显式界，并建立了该界与双变量传递函数在反对角线上的L2范数之间的等价关系，同时给出了基于线性矩阵方程的计算方法。

Conclusion: 该结果为评估和简化二次输出模型提供了一种实用的工具。

Abstract: We derive an explicit bound for the L2-L2-gain of linear time-invariant systems whose output is a quadratic function of the state and the input. Such systems appear naturally in many areas, for example for port-Hamiltonian systems, optimal-control, and stochastic problems. In case the output is purely quadratic in the state, the bound equals the L2-norm of the bivariate transfer function evaluated along the anti-diagonal $\{(s,\,-s)\mid s\in i\mathbb R\}$ of the $i \mathbb R\times i \mathbb R$ frequency domain. Further, we show how the bound can be computed by solving linear matrix equations. This result provides a practical tool for assessing and reducing quadratic-output models.

</details>


### [131] [Vehicle Routing Problem Meets Large Language Models: An Overview and Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00604)
*Xianchao Xiu,Chong Shen,Yanjiao Zhu,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLM）在车辆路径问题（VRP）及其变体中的研究进展，将现有工作按三种角色（建模者、设计者、协调者）进行系统分类，并涵盖基准测试、数据集、评估框架及对比实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VRP及其变体是NP-hard问题，实际场景中往往涉及时间窗、容量、取送货、动态请求等多重约束，建模和求解难度大。LLM提供了灵活的自然语言接口，有望降低路由优化的门槛并提升求解效率，因此有必要系统梳理该交叉领域的研究现状。

Method: 采用综述方法，首先介绍VRP基本定义、主要变体、传统求解方法及LLM相关基础概念；然后将现有LLM-driven研究按三种角色组织：建模者（将自然语言需求转为约束和建模代码）、设计者（生成启发式、算子或路径方案）、协调者（组织工具调用、多智能体协作及与神经求解器连接）；最后回顾标准基准、真实数据集、评估框架并进行两项对比实验。

Result: 系统梳理了LLM在VRP各环节的应用范式，明确了建模者、设计者、协调者三种角色的功能边界与代表性工作；总结了现有基准评估体系；通过对比实验揭示了LLM在路由优化中的能力与局限。

Conclusion: LLM在辅助路由优化方面展现出灵活性和潜力，但目前仍处于早期阶段，在求解质量、约束处理可靠性等方面有待提升。本文为智能决策、先进制造和工业自动化领域提供了结构化的参考框架。

Abstract: The vehicle routing problem (VRP) is a central optimization problem in artificial intelligence, logistics automation, transportation scheduling, and industrial decision-making. VRP and its variants are NP-hard, and practical routing tasks often combine time windows, vehicle capacities, pickup-and-delivery relations, dynamic requests, and other operational constraints, making both modeling and solving difficult. Large language models (LLMs) provide a flexible interface for routing optimization by processing natural-language requirements, generating code, reasoning over constraints, and interacting with external tools. This survey reviews LLM-driven research on VRP, covering the basic definition, main variants, major solver families, and LLM concepts needed for this topic. Existing studies are organized into three roles: modelers translate natural-language requirements into constraints and modeling code; designers generate heuristics, operators, or route plans; and coordinators organize tool calls, multi-agent collaboration, and connections with neural solvers. The survey also reviews standard benchmarks, real or near-real operational datasets, LLM-oriented evaluation frameworks, and two comparative experiments. The goal is to clarify current progress in LLM-assisted routing optimization and provide a structured reference for intelligent decision-making, advanced manufacturing, and industrial automation.

</details>


### [132] [An semidefinite programming-based $\varepsilon$-constraint method for the bi-objective single-row facility layout problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00611)
*Christof Brandstetter,Elisabeth Gaar,Markus Sinnl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了单行设施布局问题(SRFLP)的多目标版本，并基于ε-约束法开发了求解算法，该算法使用基于半定规划(SDP)的分支定界过程替代传统的整数线性规划(ILP)求解器，并提出了非二叉树分支和节点重用等增强策略，计算实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SRFLP只考虑单一加权和目标，但实际应用中往往需要同时优化多个加权和目标，因此需要引入多目标SRFLP，并在Pareto最优概念下求解。

Method: 采用ε-约束法求解双目标SRFLP；使用基于半定规划(SDP)的分支定界过程替代传统ILP黑盒求解器；提出非二叉树分支和分支定界树内节点重用等增强策略。

Result: 计算实验表明，所提出的基于SDP的ε-约束法及其增强策略能够有效求解多目标SRFLP。

Conclusion: 使用SDP松弛替代ILP松弛可以更有效地求解SRFLP，并使得更多定制化增强策略（如非二叉树分支和节点重用）成为可能，提升了多目标求解的性能。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a multi-objective version of the well-known single-row facility layout problem (SRFLP). In the SRFLP, a set of one-dimensional facilities should be placed along a single line such that the weighted sum of the center-to-center distances of each pair of facilities is minimized. In our multi-objective extension, there are multiple such weighted-sum objectives which we consider under the concept of Pareto optimality.
  We develop a solution algorithm based on the $\varepsilon$-constraint method to solve the bi-objective SRFLP. Many existing works on the $\varepsilon$-constraint method use integer linear programming (ILP) solvers in a black-box fashion for solving the problems at the individual iterations of the method. In contrast to that, we use our own branch-and-bound procedure based on semidefinite programming (SDP), as SDP relaxations are known to be more effective for solving the SRFLP than linear programming relaxations of ILPs. This allows us to propose several enhancements procedures for our $\varepsilon$-constraint approach, such as non-binary branching and reusing of nodes within the branch-and-bound trees, which are usually not possible when using black-box solvers. We present a computational study to demonstrate the effectiveness of our solution approach and its enhancements.

</details>


### [133] [Iterative graph lifting for automatic design of path-complete stability certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00637)
*Léa Ninite,Raphaël M. Jungers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图论分析的迭代算法，通过局部图提升（节点分裂）高效且简约地细化路径完备图，以逼近切换线性系统在任意切换下的联合谱半径（JSR），推导了精确JSR可达的充分条件，并利用瓶颈节点识别进行图优化，数值实验表明该方法在挑战性实例上优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切换线性系统在任意切换下的稳定性是控制理论中的基本问题，与联合谱半径（JSR）密切相关。现有的路径完备方法虽然能够通过代数稳定性证书近似JSR，但如何高效且简约地构造和优化路径完备图以获得更精确的JSR估计仍然是一个挑战。

Method: 本文提出一种迭代算法，通过图论分析底层优化问题的最优性条件，推导了给定路径完备图达到精确JSR的充分条件。当条件不满足时，通过分析活动约束诱导的子图识别瓶颈节点，随后利用局部图提升（节点分裂）技术对路径完备图进行细化，并重复该过程直至收敛。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法在所有具挑战性的测试实例上均优于现有最先进方法，具有良好的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文通过图论分析与局部图提升相结合的方式，为路径完备框架下JSR的逼近提供了一种高效且简约的迭代优化方法，在理论和实践上均取得了显著进展。

Abstract: Stability of switched linear systems under arbitrary switching is a fundamental problem in control theory, closely related to the joint spectral radius (JSR), which characterizes the worst-case growth rate of system trajectories. In this paper, we contribute to the path-complete approach for approximating the JSR. This framework constructs algebraic stability certificates using labeled directed graphs, known as path-complete graphs. These certificates can be computed via an associated optimization problem. We propose an iterative algorithm that refines path-complete graphs in an efficient and parsimonious manner. The algorithm relies on a graph-theoretic analysis of the optimality conditions of the underlying optimization problem. In particular, we derive a sufficient condition under which the exact JSR is attained by a given path-complete graph. When this condition is not satisfied, we identify bottleneck nodes by analyzing the graph induced by the active constraints. We then use this information to refine the path-complete graph via local graph lifting (node splitting), and repeat the procedure. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed approach, outperforming state-of-the-art methods on all challenging instances tested.

</details>


### [134] [Effective dynamics of the Sinkhorn algorithm in the regime of low entropy regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00665)
*Guillaume Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Sinkhorn算法在正则化参数τ→0时的极限行为，发现该算法在时间缩放t=τk下收敛到一种"冷Sinkhorn"动力学，该动力学具有类似单纯形法的性质，能在有限步内收敛到未正则化最优传输问题的解，并证明了在k=O(τ^{-1})次迭代内达到Õ(τ)的对偶次优性，优于现有分析的O(τ^{-2})界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Sinkhorn算法在极小正则化参数下表现出的分段线性对偶变量和鞍点型原始变量演化现象，揭示其极限动力学行为并改进收敛性分析。

Method: 通过渐近分析证明当τ→0时，Sinkhorn迭代在时间缩放t=τk下收敛到连续的"冷Sinkhorn"动力学；该动力学被显式刻画为未正则化问题的对偶优化动力学，具有类似单纯形法的性质。

Result: Sinkhorn算法在k=O(τ^{-1})次迭代内即可达到Õ(τ)的对偶次优性，而非现有分析中的O(τ^{-2})次迭代。

Conclusion: Sinkhorn算法在小正则化参数下具有更优的收敛速率，其极限动力学为未正则化问题提供了有限步收敛的对偶优化方法。

Abstract: The Sinkhorn algorithm is the de facto standard method for numerically solving entropy-regularized optimal transport problems over finite sets. In this work, we investigate a phenomenon arising when Sinkhorn is applied with a small regularization parameter $τ$: the evolution of the dual variables (the logarithm of the scaling factors) is approximately piecewise-linear, while the primal variables (the approximate transport plans) exhibit a saddle-to-saddle type behavior. We prove that as $τ\to 0$, the Sinkhorn iterates indeed converge to a continuous-time curve consistent with these observations, when time is rescaled as $t = τk$, and we characterize the limiting "cold Sinkhorn" dynamics explicitly. In particular, we show that it acts as a dual optimization dynamics for the unregularized problem with properties analogous to the simplex algorithm. Notably, this dynamics converges in finite time to an unregularized solution, implying a novel guarantee for the Sinkhorn algorithm itself: it achieves $\tilde{O}(τ)$ dual suboptimality in $k = O(τ^{-1})$ iterations, instead of $k = O(τ^{-2})$ as existing analyses would suggest.

</details>


### [135] [Geometric Reduced-Attitude Tracking Under a Time-Varying Conic Constraint via Smooth Reference-Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00793)
*Pedro Santos,Joel Reis,Paulo Oliveira,Carlos Silvestre*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了刚体在2-球面S²上的降阶姿态跟踪问题，提出了在时变锥约束下具有几乎全局渐近和区域指数收敛性的几何跟踪律，并通过光滑参考整形机制实现软约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决刚体在S²上的降阶姿态跟踪问题，特别是在时变锥约束下需要同时保证稳定性和约束满足的实际需求。

Method: 基于S²上的运动学模型，首先设计了几何跟踪律（以角速度为控制输入），实现无约束情况下的几乎全局渐近收敛和区域指数收敛；然后引入光滑参考整形机制，调整期望方向以满足时变锥约束，同时保持控制器所需的光滑性。

Result: 所提方法实现了光滑连续反馈，在保留无约束控制器稳定性保证的前提下，以软约束形式满足原始约束。仿真结果验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于偏好确定性行为、光滑控制作用和强稳定性保证而非硬约束满足的应用场景。

Abstract: This letter studies reduced-attitude tracking for a rigid body on the 2-sphere S2 under a time-varying conic constraint. Using a kinematic model on S2, we first propose a geometric tracking law that guarantees almostglobal asymptotic and regionally exponential convergence in the unconstrained case, where the angular velocity serves as the control input. We then introduce a smooth reference-shaping mechanism that adjusts the desired direction so that the reference provided to the controller satisfies the time-varying conic constraint while preserving the smoothness required by the tracking law. The resulting approach yields smooth continuous feedback and retains the stability guarantees of the unconstrained controller, albeit at the expense of enforcing a soft version of the original constraint. Simulation results illustrate the effectiveness of the method and highlight its suitability for applications where deterministic behavior, smooth control action, and strong stability guarantees are preferred over hard constraint satisfaction.

</details>


### [136] [Optimal control problem for reflected McKean--Vlasov stochastic differential equations with Poisson jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00799)
*Wenrui Lu,Kai Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类带泊松跳的反射McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程的最优松弛控制问题，证明了最优松弛控制的存在性，并在Roxin凸性条件下证明了严格最优控制的存在性，以及松弛控制可由严格控制序列逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考虑带泊松跳的一维反射McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程的最优松弛控制问题，由于跳项的存在使得状态过程属于Skorokhod空间，紧性证明和极限过渡更为复杂。

Method: 在Lipschitz条件和适当增长条件下建立状态过程和反射过程的一致矩估计，利用Aldous紧性准则、Skorokhod映射的连续性以及随机积分的稳定性结果证明最优松弛控制的存在性。

Result: 证明了最优松弛控制的存在性；在Roxin凸性条件下证明了严格最优控制的存在性；一般情况下证明了松弛控制可由严格控制序列逼近。

Conclusion: 对于带泊松跳的反射McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程，松弛控制框架是有效的，且在凸性条件下可退化为严格最优控制。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the optimal relaxed control problem for a class of one-dimensional reflected McKean--Vlasov stochastic differential equations with Poisson jumps. Due to the presence of the jump term, the state process generally belongs to the Skorokhod space $D([0,T],\Rp)$, which makes the proof of tightness and the passage to the limit more complicated. Under Lipschitz conditions and suitable growth conditions, we establish uniform moment estimates for the state process and the reflecting process. Then, by using Aldous' tightness criterion, the continuity of the Skorokhod map, and the stability results for stochastic integrals, we prove the existence of an optimal relaxed control. Furthermore, under the Roxin convexity condition, we prove the existence of a strict optimal control. In the general case, we show that relaxed controls can be approximated by a sequence of strict controls.

</details>


### [137] [Experiment Design for Set-membership Identification: From Prior Knowledge to Universal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00844)
*Amir Shakouri,Henk J. van Waarde,M. Kanat Camlibel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了未知线性时不变系统的输入信号设计问题，提出了通用输入概念及其设计方法，推广了基于Willems基本引理的实验设计方法，在多种先验知识下实现了更优的样本效率，并探讨了有噪环境下精确识别的实验设计条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于持续激励输入的实验设计方法局限于系统可控性这一先验知识，无法有效利用其他类型的先验信息。本文旨在设计更通用的输入信号，使其在任意符合先验知识的系统上都能产生适合准确辨识的数据，并探索不同先验知识类型对实验设计的影响。

Method: 提出“通用输入”概念，即对任何符合先验知识的系统都能产生适合准确辨识的数据的输入信号；考虑无噪声和带ℓ∞-界噪声两种设置；推广Willems基本引理的持续激励输入方法；设计新的通用输入构造方法。

Result: 对于非可控性先验知识，存在比持续激励输入更优的通用输入，在样本效率等方面表现更好；探讨了某些先验知识类型可在有噪环境下实现精确辨识的实验设计。

Conclusion: 通用输入设计方法能更好地利用不同先验知识，突破传统持续激励输入的局限，在样本效率等方面获得提升，并为有噪环境下的精确辨识提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of designing input signals for an unknown linear time-invariant system in such a way that the resulting data, within a finite horizon, is suitable for identification with a desired accuracy. We consider both noise-free and noisy settings with $\ell_\infty$--bounded noise models. We will take into account general prior knowledge of the system parameters. Central in our study is the concept of universal inputs. An input is called universal for identification if, when applied to any system complying with the prior knowledge, it yields data suitable for accurate identification. We provide new methods for designing such universal inputs. Our results generalize the experiment design approach based on Willems et al.'s fundamental lemma that relies on persistently exciting inputs, and that is limited to prior knowledge on controllability. It turns out that for other types of prior knowledge, there exist universal inputs that outperform the persistently exciting ones, e.g., in terms of sample efficiency. Moreover, we investigate types of prior knowledge that enable experiment design for exact identification in the presence of noise.

</details>


### [138] [On directional local minimality and directional optimality conditions in nonsmooth optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00882)
*Timm Baake,Patrick Mehlitz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对下半连续函数的无约束极小化问题，利用一阶/二阶次导数、方向极限次微分和方向近端次微分，基于方向局部极小性概念，推导出必要且充分的一阶和二阶最优性条件，进而得到传统无方向局部极小性的最优性条件，并通过实例展示其比经典条件更精细的分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的非光滑优化最优性条件（如Clarke次微分）在处理某些非凸、非光滑问题时过于粗糙，无法区分不同方向上的局部行为。本文旨在引入方向性分析工具，提供更精细的最优性刻画。

Method: 利用一阶次导数和二阶次导数、方向极限次微分以及方向近端次微分等变分分析工具，基于"方向局部极小性"这一新概念，推导出方向性的一阶和二阶最优性条件（必要与充分），并将其转化为关于临界方向的传统无方向局部极小性条件。

Result: 得到了基于方向导数的最优性必要条件和充分条件，并建立了从方向局部极小到传统局部极小的桥梁；通过实例验证了所提条件比经典无方向最优性条件具有更强的判别能力。

Conclusion: 引入方向性分析工具可以显著提升非光滑优化最优性条件的刻画精度，为更复杂的非凸非光滑优化问题提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This paper considers the unconstrained minimization of a lower semicontinuous function. Exploiting first and second subderivatives, directional limiting subdifferentials, and directional proximal subdifferentials, necessary and sufficient first- and second-order optimality conditions are derived that build upon the recently introduced notion of directional local minimality. These results then also yield optimality conditions for conventional nondirectional local minimality which are stated in terms of so-called critical directions and variational objects depending on them. Illustrative examples show that the derived conditions allow for a finer analysis than classical nondirectional optimality conditions.

</details>


### [139] [Influence of Routing and Speed Limits on Optimal Solutions in Traffic Emission Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00944)
*Marc-André Bach,Simone Göttlich,Alena Ulke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了交通排放模型中路由策略和限速策略对最优解的影响，发现在小型路网上限速策略在减少排放和提高交通效率方面均优于路由策略，多目标优化表明包含限速的控制集能产生更优的Pareto解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究路由策略和限速策略对交通排放模型最优解的影响，寻求同时最大化交通效率和最小化空气污染的交通管理方案。

Method: 基于一阶宏观交通模型与对流-扩散耦合模型，构建单目标和多目标优化问题，比较三种控制场景：仅优化路由策略、仅优化限速策略、同时优化两者。

Result: 限速策略在减少排放和提升交通效率方面始终优于路由策略；多目标优化显示两个目标之间存在权衡，包含限速的控制集得到的Pareto最优解严格优于仅路由控制。

Conclusion: 限速策略是比路由策略更有效的交通排放控制手段，为平衡交通流动性与环境目标的交通管理提供了定量指导。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of routing strategies and speed limit policies on optimal solutions in traffic emission models. Building on a first-order macroscopic traffic model coupled with an advection-diffusion model, we formulate single- and multi-objective optimization problems to simultaneously maximize traffic efficiency and minimize air pollution. We compare three control scenarios: optimizing only the routing strategy, optimizing only the speed limit policy, and optimizing both simultaneously. Numerical experiments on a small road network demonstrate that speed limit policies consistently achieve larger reductions in emissions and greater gains in traffic efficiency than routing strategies. Multi-objective optimization reveals the trade-off between the two goals and confirms that including speed limits in the control set yields Pareto-optimal solutions that are strictly superior to those obtained by routing control only. Our results provide quantitative guidance for traffic management seeking to balance mobility and environmental objectives.

</details>


### [140] [Computationally Efficient Near-Optimal Control for Current Ripple Reduction and Optimization of Three-Phase Motors via LMIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01215)
*Huu-Thinh Do,Trung B. Tran,Jing Sun,Ilya Kolmanovsky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对永磁同步电机最优控制中非线性与离散控制集的挑战，提出一种基于线性矩阵不等式（LMI）的无限时域值函数二次参数化近似方法，通过迭代Bellman不等式构造凸优化问题，离线高效求解后作为在线一步最优控制的尾端代价，在开关损耗与电流纹波间取得良好权衡，计算成本远低于有限控制集MPC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三相永磁同步电机（PMSM）的最优控制因其非线性和控制集的离散特性而具有挑战性。现有方法要么依赖混合整数轨迹优化，要么需要计算密集的值迭代过程，计算成本高。

Method: 提出一种基于线性矩阵不等式（LMI）的方法：采用二次参数化形式逼近无限时域值函数，结合迭代Bellman不等式，将问题转化为可解的凸规划问题。离线高效计算该值函数，在线作为一步最优控制律中的尾端代价使用。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在开关损耗与电流纹波间实现了有利的权衡，性能与有限控制集MPC相当，但计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的LMI-based方法为PMSM最优控制提供了一种高效且性能良好的离线求解方案，兼顾开关损耗与电流纹波的权衡，计算效率优于传统MPC方法。

Abstract: The optimal control of three-phase permanent-magnet synchronous motors (PMSMs) is challenging due to their nonlinearity and the discrete nature of the control set. Existing approaches either rely on mixed-integer trajectory optimization or require computationally intensive value-iteration procedures. This paper proposes a Linear Matrix Inequality (LMI)-based method for approximating the infinite-horizon value function using a quadratic parameterization and iterated Bellman inequalities, yielding a tractable convex program. The computed function can be obtained efficiently offline and used online as a tail cost in a horizon-one optimal control law. Simulation results show that the proposed approach achieves a favorable trade-off between switching effort and current ripple, with performance comparable to that of finite-control-set MPC but with a significantly lower computational cost.

</details>
